=== FILE: \src\app\pages\add-review\add-review.component.ts ===
import { Component, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormBuilder, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { catchError, map, of, switchMap } from 'rxjs';
import { FrontOfficeLayoutComponent } from '../../layouts/front-office-layout/front-office-layout.component';
import { ReviewsTrustService } from '../../core/services/reviews-trust/reviews-trust.service';
import {
  EntityType,
  ReviewEntity,
  ReviewTarget,
} from '../../shared/models/reviews/reviews-trust.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-add-review',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ReactiveFormsModule, RouterModule, FrontOfficeLayoutComponent],
  templateUrl: './add-review.component.html',
  styleUrl: './add-review.component.scss',
})
export class AddReviewComponent {
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);

  readonly stars = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];
  hoverRating = 0;
  isSubmitting = false;
  successMessage = '';
  errorMessage = '';

  readonly form = this.fb.nonNullable.group({
    targetType: ['ORGANISATION' as ReviewTarget, Validators.required],
    rating: [5, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1), Validators.max(5)]],
    body: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(5)]],
  });

  constructor(private readonly reviewsTrustService: ReviewsTrustService) {}

  setRating(value: number): void {
    this.form.controls.rating.setValue(value);
    this.form.controls.rating.markAsTouched();
  }

  displayStarValue(star: number): boolean {
    const preview = this.hoverRating > 0 ? this.hoverRating : this.form.controls.rating.value;
    return star <= preview;
  }

  submit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) {
      this.form.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.isSubmitting = true;
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.errorMessage = '';

    const raw = this.form.getRawValue();
    const entityType: EntityType = raw.targetType;

    this.resolveReviewContext(raw.targetType)
      .pipe(
        switchMap((context) =>
          this.reviewsTrustService.createReview({
            authorId: context.authorId,
            targetId: context.targetId,
            targetType: raw.targetType,
            rating: Number(raw.rating),
            body: raw.body.trim(),
            opportunityId: null,
          }),
        ),
        switchMap((created) =>
          this.reviewsTrustService.recalculateTrustScore(created.targetId, entityType),
        ),
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage =
            'Review submitted successfully. Trust score was updated.';
          this.isSubmitting = false;
          this.hoverRating = 0;
          this.form.reset({
            targetType: raw.targetType,
            rating: 5,
            body: '',
          });
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.errorMessage = this.extractErrorMessage(err);
          this.isSubmitting = false;
        },
      });
  }

  private resolveReviewContext(targetType: ReviewTarget) {
    return this.reviewsTrustService.getAllReviews().pipe(
      catchError(() => of([] as ReviewEntity[])),
      map((items) => {
        const sameType = items.find((item) => item.targetType === targetType);
        const fallback = items[0];

        return {
          authorId: this.resolveAuthorId(sameType ?? fallback),
          targetId: this.resolveTargetId(targetType, sameType ?? fallback),
        };
      }),
    );
  }

  private extractErrorMessage(err: unknown): string {
    console.error('Submit Error:', err);
    if (err instanceof Error) {
      return `Error: ${err.message}`;
    }
    if (typeof err === 'object' && err !== null) {
      const withError = err as any;
      if (withError.error?.message) {
        return withError.error.message;
      }
      if (withError.message) {
        return withError.message;
      }
      return `API Error: ${JSON.stringify(err)}`;
    }
    return 'Unable to submit the review right now. Please try again.';
  }

  private resolveAuthorId(item?: ReviewEntity): number {
    const userId = Number(localStorage.getItem('userId'));
    return userId > 0 ? userId : 1;
  }

  private resolveTargetId(targetType: ReviewTarget, item?: ReviewEntity): number {
    if (
      item &&
      item.targetType === targetType &&
      typeof item.targetId === 'number' &&
      item.targetId > 0
    ) {
      return item.targetId;
    }

    if (item && typeof item.targetId === 'number' && item.targetId > 0) {
      return item.targetId;
    }

    return 1;
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\admin\admin-coordination\admin-coordination.component.ts ===
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import {
  CoordinationService,
  HelpRequest,
  TaskAssignment,
} from '../../../core/services/coordination.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-admin-coordination',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './admin-coordination.component.html',
})
export class AdminCoordinationComponent implements OnInit {
  requests: HelpRequest[] = [];
  filtered: HelpRequest[] = [];
  assignmentsMap: Record<number, TaskAssignment[]> = {};
  isLoading = true;
  errorMessage = '';
  expandedId: number | null = null;
  statusFilter = 'ALL';
  typeFilter = 'ALL';

  statuses = [
    'ALL',
    'OPEN',
    'ASSIGNED',
    'IN_PROGRESS',
    'RESOLVED',
    'CANCELLED',
  ];
  types = [
    'ALL',
    'RESCUE',
    'MEDICAL',
    'EVACUATION',
    'SUPPLY',
    'SHELTER',
    'TECHNICAL',
  ];

  statusBadge: Record<string, string> = {
    OPEN: 'bg-secondary/10 text-secondary border-secondary/20',
    ASSIGNED: 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/20',
    IN_PROGRESS: 'bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400 border-orange-500/20',
    RESOLVED: 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/20',
    CANCELLED: 'bg-error/10 text-error border-error/20',
  };

  aStatusBadge: Record<string, string> = {
    ASSIGNED: 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400',
    ACCEPTED: 'bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400',
    COMPLETED: 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400',
    REJECTED: 'bg-error/10 text-error',
    ABANDONED: 'bg-slate-500/10 text-slate-400',
  };

  urgencyColor = [
    '',
    'text-secondary',
    'text-secondary',
    'text-amber-400',
    'text-orange-400',
    'text-error',
  ];

  constructor(private svc: CoordinationService) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.load();
  }

  load(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.svc.getAllRequests().subscribe({
      next: (d) => {
        this.requests = d;
        this.applyFilters();
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Backend unreachable.';
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
    });
  }

  applyFilters(): void {
    this.filtered = this.requests.filter((r) => {
      const s = this.statusFilter === 'ALL' || r.status === this.statusFilter;
      const t = this.typeFilter === 'ALL' || r.type === this.typeFilter;
      return s && t;
    });
  }

  toggle(id: number): void {
    if (this.expandedId === id) {
      this.expandedId = null;
      return;
    }
    this.expandedId = id;
    if (!this.assignmentsMap[id]) {
      this.svc
        .getAssignmentsByRequest(id)
        .subscribe((a) => (this.assignmentsMap[id] = a));
    }
  }

  updateRequestStatus(id: number, status: string): void {
    const r = this.requests.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (!r) return;
    this.svc.updateRequest(id, { ...r, status }).subscribe({
      next: (updated) => {
        const i = this.requests.findIndex((x) => x.id === id);
        this.requests[i] = updated;
        this.applyFilters();
      },
    });
  }

  updateAssignStatus(id: number, status: string, reqId: number): void {
    this.svc.updateAssignmentStatus(id, status).subscribe(() => {
      this.svc
        .getAssignmentsByRequest(reqId)
        .subscribe((a) => (this.assignmentsMap[reqId] = a));
    });
  }

  deleteRequest(id: number): void {
    if (!confirm('Delete this request and all assignments?')) return;
    this.svc.deleteRequest(id).subscribe({ next: () => this.load() });
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';

import { IncidentService, IncidentReport, StatusHistory } from '../../../core/services/incident.service';







@Component({
  selector: 'app-admin-incidents',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './admin-incidents.component.html',
})
export class AdminIncidentsComponent implements OnInit {
  reports: IncidentReport[] = [];
  filtered: IncidentReport[] = [];
  isLoading = true;
  errorMessage = '';
  activeFilter = 'ALL';
  expandedId: number | null = null;
  statusFilter = 'ALL';
  severityFilter = 'ALL';

  historyMap: Record<number, StatusHistory[]> = {};
  loadingHistory = false;

  statuses = ['ALL', 'PENDING', 'VERIFIED', 'REJECTED', 'ESCALATED'];
  severities = ['ALL', 'LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'CRITICAL'];

  credColor(score: number | undefined): string {
    if (!score) return 'text-on-surface-variant';
    if (score >= 0.8) return 'text-secondary';
    if (score >= 0.5) return 'text-amber-400';
    return 'text-error';
  }

  severityBadge: Record<string, string> = {
    LOW: 'bg-secondary/10 text-secondary border-secondary/20',
    MEDIUM: 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/20',
    HIGH: 'bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400 border-orange-500/20',
    CRITICAL: 'bg-error/20 text-error border-error/30',
  };

  statusBadge: Record<string, string> = {
    PENDING: 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/20',
    VERIFIED: 'bg-secondary/10 text-secondary border-secondary/20',
    REJECTED: 'bg-error/10 text-error border-error/20',
    ESCALATED: 'bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400 border-orange-500/20',
  };

  constructor(private svc: IncidentService) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.load();
  }

  load(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.svc.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (d) => {
        this.reports = d;
        this.applyFilters();
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Backend unreachable.';
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
    });
  }

  applyFilters(): void {
    this.filtered = this.reports.filter((r) => {
      const s = this.statusFilter === 'ALL' || r.status === this.statusFilter;
      const v =
        this.severityFilter === 'ALL' || r.severity === this.severityFilter;
      return s && v;
    });
  }

  updateStatus(id: number, status: string): void {
    this.svc.updateStatus(id, status).subscribe({
      next: updated => {
        const i = this.reports.findIndex(x => x.id === id);
        if (i !== -1) this.reports[i] = updated as any;
        this.applyFilters();
      },
      error: () => this.errorMessage = 'Update failed.'
    });
  }


  delete(id: number): void {
    if (!confirm('Delete this report?')) return;
    this.svc.delete(id).subscribe({ next: () => this.load() });
  }


  toggle(id: number): void {
    if (this.expandedId === id) { this.expandedId = null; return; }
    this.expandedId = id;
    if (!this.historyMap[id]) {
      this.svc.getHistory(id).subscribe(h => this.historyMap[id] = h);
    }
  }

  getTimelineIcon(status: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      PENDING:   'schedule',
      VERIFIED:  'check',
      REJECTED:  'close',
      ESCALATED: 'arrow_upward',
    };
    return map[status] || 'circle';
  }

  getTimelineDotStyle(status: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      PENDING:   'background:rgba(245,158,11,0.8)',
      VERIFIED:  'background:rgba(105,156,255,0.8)',
      REJECTED:  'background:rgba(255,110,132,0.8)',
      ESCALATED: 'background:rgba(249,115,22,0.8)',
    };
    return map[status] || 'background:rgba(255,255,255,0.2)';
  }

  getStatusStyle(status: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      PENDING:   'background:rgba(245,158,11,0.15);color:#fbbf24',
      VERIFIED:  'background:rgba(105,156,255,0.15);color:#699cff',
      REJECTED:  'background:rgba(255,110,132,0.15);color:#ff6e84',
      ESCALATED: 'background:rgba(249,115,22,0.15);color:#fb923c',
    };
    return map[status] || 'background:rgba(255,255,255,0.05);color:#94a3b8';
  }



}

=== FILE: \src\app\pages\admin\backoffice-resources\backoffice-resources.component.ts ===
import { Component, OnInit, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormBuilder, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Observable, map, switchMap, of } from 'rxjs';
import { ResourceShelterService } from '../../../core/services/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.service';
import {
  ResourceCondition,
  ResourceEntity,
  ResourcePayload,
  ResourceStatus,
  ResourceType,
} from '../../../shared/models/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-backoffice-resources',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    ReactiveFormsModule,
    FormsModule,
  ],
  templateUrl: './backoffice-resources.component.html',
  styleUrl: './backoffice-resources.component.scss',
})
export class BackofficeResourcesComponent implements OnInit {
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);

  resources: ResourceEntity[] = [];
  isLoading = false;
  isSaving = false;
  editingId: number | null = null;
  successMessage = '';
  errorMessage = '';
  searchTerm = '';
  currentPage = 1;
  pageSize = 5;
  readonly pageSizeOptions = [5, 10, 20];

  readonly types: ResourceType[] = [
    'VEHICLE',
    'MEDICAL_KIT',
    'FOOD_SUPPLY',
    'EQUIPMENT',
    'GENERATOR',
    'BOAT',
  ];

  readonly conditions: ResourceCondition[] = ['GOOD', 'FAIR', 'POOR'];
  readonly statuses: ResourceStatus[] = [
    'AVAILABLE',
    'DEPLOYED',
    'RESERVED',
    'MAINTENANCE',
  ];

  readonly form = this.fb.nonNullable.group({
    ownerId: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    type: ['VEHICLE' as ResourceType, Validators.required],
    name: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(2)]],
    quantity: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(0)]],
    condition: ['GOOD' as ResourceCondition, Validators.required],
    locationUrl: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.pattern('https?://.+')]],
    status: ['AVAILABLE' as ResourceStatus, Validators.required],
    allocatedToRequestId: [''],
  });

  constructor(private readonly resourceShelterService: ResourceShelterService) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadResources();
  }

  get filteredResources(): ResourceEntity[] {
    const term = this.searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!term) {
      return this.resources;
    }

    return this.resources.filter((item) => {
      const ownerId = this.getOwnerId(item);
      const target = [
        String(item.resourceId),
        item.name,
        item.type,
        item.status,
        String(item.quantity),
        String(ownerId),
      ]
        .join(' ')
        .toLowerCase();
      return target.includes(term);
    });
  }

  get totalItems(): number {
    return this.filteredResources.length;
  }

  get totalPages(): number {
    const pages = Math.ceil(this.totalItems / this.pageSize);
    if (pages > 0) {
      return pages;
    }
    return 1;
  }

  get paginatedResources(): ResourceEntity[] {
    const start = (this.currentPage - 1) * this.pageSize;
    const end = start + this.pageSize;
    return this.filteredResources.slice(start, end);
  }

  get pageStart(): number {
    if (this.totalItems === 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    return (this.currentPage - 1) * this.pageSize + 1;
  }

  get pageEnd(): number {
    if (this.totalItems === 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    const end = this.currentPage * this.pageSize;
    if (end > this.totalItems) {
      return this.totalItems;
    }
    return end;
  }

  loadResources(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.resourceShelterService.getAllResources().subscribe({
      next: (items) => {
        this.resources = items;
        this.normalizePage();
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Impossible de charger les resources.';
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
    });
  }

  submit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) {
      this.form.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.isSaving = true;

    const raw = this.form.getRawValue();
    this.resolveOwnerId(Number(raw.ownerId)).subscribe({
      next: (resolvedOwnerId) => {
        const payload: ResourcePayload = {
          ownerId: resolvedOwnerId,
          type: raw.type,
          name: raw.name.trim(),
          quantity: Number(raw.quantity),
          condition: raw.condition,
          locationUrl: raw.locationUrl.trim(),
          status: raw.status,
          allocatedToRequestId: this.parseOptionalNumber(raw.allocatedToRequestId),
        };

        if (this.editingId === null) {
          this.resourceShelterService.createResource(payload).subscribe({
            next: () => {
              this.successMessage = 'Resource creee avec succes.';
              this.afterMutation();
            },
            error: (err) => {
              this.errorMessage = this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'Erreur creation resource.');
              this.isSaving = false;
            },
          });
          return;
        }

        this.resourceShelterService.updateResource(this.editingId, payload).subscribe({
          next: () => {
            this.successMessage = 'Resource modifiee avec succes.';
            this.afterMutation();
          },
          error: (err) => {
            this.errorMessage = this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'Erreur modification resource.');
            this.isSaving = false;
          },
        });
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage = this.extractErrorMessage(
          err,
          'Impossible de resoudre un utilisateur backend pour resource.',
        );
        this.isSaving = false;
      },
    });
  }

  startEdit(item: ResourceEntity): void {
    this.editingId = item.resourceId;
    this.form.patchValue({
      ownerId: this.getOwnerId(item),
      type: item.type,
      name: item.name,
      quantity: item.quantity,
      condition: item.condition,
      locationUrl: item.locationUrl,
      status: item.status,
      allocatedToRequestId: item.allocatedToRequestId
        ? String(item.allocatedToRequestId)
        : '',
    });
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.errorMessage = '';
  }

  cancelEdit(): void {
    this.editingId = null;
    this.form.reset({
      ownerId: 1,
      type: 'VEHICLE',
      name: '',
      quantity: 1,
      condition: 'GOOD',
      locationUrl: '',
      status: 'AVAILABLE',
      allocatedToRequestId: '',
    });
  }

  remove(item: ResourceEntity): void {
    const ok = window.confirm(`Supprimer la resource #${item.resourceId} ?`);
    if (!ok) {
      return;
    }

    this.resourceShelterService.deleteResource(item.resourceId).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.successMessage = 'Resource supprimee avec succes.';
        this.loadResources();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Erreur suppression resource.';
      },
    });
  }

  exportResourcesExcel(): void {
    if (this.resources.length === 0) {
      this.errorMessage = 'No resources to export.';
      return;
    }

    const rowsHtml = this.resources
      .map((item, index) => {
        const cells = [
          String(item.resourceId),
          item.name,
          item.type,
          String(item.quantity),
          item.status,
          item.condition,
          String(this.getOwnerId(item)),
          item.allocatedToRequestId !== null && item.allocatedToRequestId !== undefined
            ? String(item.allocatedToRequestId)
            : '-',
          item.locationUrl,
          item.lastUpdatedAt ?? '-',
        ];

        const cellHtml = cells
          .map((cell) => `<td>${this.escapeHtml(cell)}</td>`)
          .join('');

        const zebraClass = index % 2 === 0 ? 'row-even' : 'row-odd';
        return `<tr class="${zebraClass}">${cellHtml}</tr>`;
      })
      .join('');

    const htmlContent = `
<html>
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <style>
      body { font-family: Calibri, Arial, sans-serif; }
      h2 { margin: 0 0 12px 0; color: #1f4e78; }
      .meta { margin: 0 0 14px 0; color: #666; font-size: 12px; }
      table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; }
      th, td {
        border: 1px solid #d9e1f2;
        padding: 8px 10px;
        font-size: 12px;
        vertical-align: middle;
      }
      th {
        background: #2f5597;
        color: #fff;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
      }
      .row-even { background: #edf3ff; }
      .row-odd { background: #ffffff; }
      td:nth-child(4), td:nth-child(7), td:nth-child(8) { text-align: center; }
      td:nth-child(9) { color: #1a73e8; }
    </style>
  </head>
  <body>
    <h2>Resources Export</h2>
    <p class="meta">Generated at: ${this.escapeHtml(new Date().toLocaleString())}</p>
    <table>
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th>ID</th>
          <th>Name</th>
          <th>Type</th>
          <th>Quantity</th>
          <th>Status</th>
          <th>Condition</th>
          <th>Owner ID</th>
          <th>Allocated Request ID</th>
          <th>Location URL</th>
          <th>Last Updated</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        ${rowsHtml}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </body>
</html>`;

    const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + htmlContent], {
      type: 'application/vnd.ms-excel;charset=utf-8;',
    });

    const now = new Date();
    const fileName = `resources-export-${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}.xls`;

    const link = document.createElement('a');
    const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    link.href = objectUrl;
    link.setAttribute('download', fileName);
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);

    this.successMessage = 'Resources exported successfully.';
    this.errorMessage = '';
  }

  onSearchChange(): void {
    this.currentPage = 1;
    this.normalizePage();
  }

  onPageSizeChange(value: string): void {
    const parsed = Number(value);
    if (Number.isNaN(parsed) || parsed <= 0) {
      return;
    }

    this.pageSize = parsed;
    this.currentPage = 1;
    this.normalizePage();
  }

  prevPage(): void {
    if (this.currentPage <= 1) {
      return;
    }
    this.currentPage -= 1;
    this.normalizePage();
  }

  nextPage(): void {
    if (this.currentPage >= this.totalPages) {
      return;
    }
    this.currentPage += 1;
    this.normalizePage();
  }

  getOwnerId(item: ResourceEntity): number {
    if (item.owner && typeof item.owner.userId === 'number') {
      return item.owner.userId;
    }
    if (item.owner && typeof item.owner.id === 'number') {
      return item.owner.id;
    }
    return 1;
  }

  private afterMutation(): void {
    this.isSaving = false;
    this.cancelEdit();
    this.loadResources();
  }

  private parseOptionalNumber(value: string | number | null | undefined): number | null {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
      return null;
    }

    if (typeof value === 'number') {
      return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
    }

    const normalized = value.trim();
    if (!normalized) {
      return null;
    }

    const parsed = Number(normalized);
    return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
  }

  private escapeHtml(value: string): string {
    return value
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
  }

  private normalizePage(): void {
    if (this.currentPage < 1) {
      this.currentPage = 1;
    }

    const max = this.totalPages;
    if (this.currentPage > max) {
      this.currentPage = max;
    }
  }

  private resolveOwnerId(requestedId: number): Observable<number> {
    const userId = Number(localStorage.getItem('userId'));
    return of(requestedId > 0 ? requestedId : (userId > 0 ? userId : 1));
  }

  private extractErrorMessage(err: unknown, fallback: string): string {
    if (typeof err === 'object' && err !== null) {
      const withError = err as { error?: { message?: string } };
      if (withError.error && withError.error.message) {
        return withError.error.message;
      }
    }
    return fallback;
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormBuilder, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Observable, map, switchMap, of } from 'rxjs';
import { ResourceShelterService } from '../../../core/services/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.service';
import {
  ShelterEntity,
  ShelterOccupantEntity,
  ShelterPayload,
  ShelterStatus,
} from '../../../shared/models/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-backoffice-shelters',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    ReactiveFormsModule,
    FormsModule,
  ],
  templateUrl: './backoffice-shelters.component.html',
  styleUrl: './backoffice-shelters.component.scss',
})
export class BackofficeSheltersComponent implements OnInit {
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);
  private readonly uiToApiStatusMap: Record<UiShelterStatus, ShelterStatus> = {
    AVAILABLE: 'OPEN',
    FULL: 'FULL',
  };
  private readonly apiToUiStatusMap: Record<ShelterStatus, UiShelterStatus> = {
    OPEN: 'AVAILABLE',
    FULL: 'FULL',
    CLOSED: 'FULL',
    PREPARING: 'AVAILABLE',
  };

  shelters: ShelterEntity[] = [];
  isLoading = false;
  isSaving = false;
  editingId: number | null = null;
  successMessage = '';
  errorMessage = '';
  searchTerm = '';
  currentPage = 1;
  pageSize = 5;
  readonly pageSizeOptions = [5, 10, 20];

  readonly statuses: UiShelterStatus[] = ['AVAILABLE', 'FULL'];
  readonly shelterTypes: UiShelterType[] = ['LOCAL', 'MEDICAL'];

  readonly form = this.fb.nonNullable.group({
    name: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(2)]],
    locationUrl: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.pattern('https?://.+')]],
    address: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(3)]],
    capacity: [10, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    managerId: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    shelterType: ['LOCAL' as UiShelterType, Validators.required],
    status: ['AVAILABLE' as UiShelterStatus, Validators.required],
  });

  constructor(private readonly resourceShelterService: ResourceShelterService) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadShelters();
  }

  get filteredShelters(): ShelterEntity[] {
    const orderedShelters = [...this.shelters].sort(
      (a, b) => b.shelterId - a.shelterId,
    );

    const term = this.searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!term) {
      return orderedShelters;
    }

    return orderedShelters.filter((item) => {
      const target = [
        item.name,
        item.address,
        item.locationUrl,
        this.managerIdOf(item),
        this.availabilityStatus(item),
        String(item.capacity),
        String(this.displayOccupancy(item)),
        this.shelterTypeLabel(item),
      ]
        .join(' ')
        .toLowerCase();

      return target.includes(term);
    });
  }

  get totalItems(): number {
    return this.filteredShelters.length;
  }

  get totalPages(): number {
    const pages = Math.ceil(this.totalItems / this.pageSize);
    if (pages > 0) {
      return pages;
    }
    return 1;
  }

  get paginatedShelters(): ShelterEntity[] {
    const start = (this.currentPage - 1) * this.pageSize;
    const end = start + this.pageSize;
    return this.filteredShelters.slice(start, end);
  }

  get pageStart(): number {
    if (this.totalItems === 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    return (this.currentPage - 1) * this.pageSize + 1;
  }

  get pageEnd(): number {
    if (this.totalItems === 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    const end = this.currentPage * this.pageSize;
    if (end > this.totalItems) {
      return this.totalItems;
    }
    return end;
  }

  loadShelters(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.resourceShelterService.getAllShelters().subscribe({
      next: (items) => {
        this.shelters = items;
        this.normalizePage();
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Impossible de charger les shelters.';
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
    });
  }

  submit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) {
      this.form.markAllAsTouched();
      this.errorMessage = 'Formulaire invalide. Verifiez Name, Address (min 3), Location URL, Capacity, Status et Type.';
      return;
    }

    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.isSaving = true;

    const raw = this.form.getRawValue();
    this.resolveManagerId(Number(raw.managerId)).subscribe({
      next: (resolvedManagerId) => {
        const payload: ShelterPayload = {
          name: raw.name.trim(),
          locationUrl: raw.locationUrl.trim(),
          address: raw.address.trim(),
          capacity: Number(raw.capacity),
          managerId: resolvedManagerId,
          amenities: [`TYPE:${raw.shelterType}`],
          status: this.toApiStatus(raw.status),
        };

        if (this.editingId === null) {
          this.resourceShelterService.createShelter(payload).subscribe({
            next: () => {
              this.successMessage = 'Shelter cree avec succes.';
              this.afterMutation();
            },
            error: (err) => {
              this.errorMessage = this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'Erreur creation shelter.');
              this.isSaving = false;
            },
          });
          return;
        }

        this.resourceShelterService.updateShelter(this.editingId, payload).subscribe({
          next: () => {
            this.successMessage = 'Shelter modifie avec succes.';
            this.afterMutation();
          },
          error: (err) => {
            this.errorMessage = this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'Erreur modification shelter.');
            this.isSaving = false;
          },
        });
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage = this.extractErrorMessage(
          err,
          'Impossible de resoudre un utilisateur backend pour shelter.',
        );
        this.isSaving = false;
      },
    });
  }

  startEdit(item: ShelterEntity): void {
    this.editingId = item.shelterId;
    this.form.patchValue({
      name: item.name,
      locationUrl: item.locationUrl,
      address: item.address,
      capacity: item.capacity,
      managerId: 1,
      shelterType: this.extractShelterType(item),
      status: this.toUiStatus(item.status),
    });
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.errorMessage = '';
  }

  cancelEdit(): void {
    this.editingId = null;
    this.form.reset({
      name: '',
      locationUrl: '',
      address: '',
      capacity: 10,
      managerId: 1,
      shelterType: 'LOCAL',
      status: 'AVAILABLE',
    });
  }

  remove(item: ShelterEntity): void {
    const ok = window.confirm(`Supprimer le shelter #${item.shelterId} ?`);
    if (!ok) {
      return;
    }

    this.resourceShelterService.deleteShelter(item.shelterId).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.successMessage = 'Shelter supprime avec succes.';
        this.loadShelters();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Erreur suppression shelter.';
      },
    });
  }

  checkIn(item: ShelterEntity): void {
    const current = this.displayOccupancy(item);
    if (current >= item.capacity) {
      return;
    }

    this.resolveManagerId(this.occupantUserId(item))
      .pipe(
        switchMap((resolvedUserId) =>
          this.resourceShelterService.checkInShelter({
            shelterId: item.shelterId,
            userId: resolvedUserId,
          }),
        ),
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage = 'Occupancy updated successfully.';
          this.errorMessage = '';
          this.loadShelters();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.errorMessage = this.extractErrorMessage(
            err,
            'Unable to check in occupant.',
          );
        },
      });
  }

  checkOut(item: ShelterEntity): void {
    const current = this.displayOccupancy(item);
    if (current <= 0) {
      return;
    }

    this.resourceShelterService
      .getShelterOccupantsByShelter(item.shelterId)
      .pipe(
        map((records) => this.latestActiveOccupant(records)),
        switchMap((activeRecord) => {
          if (!activeRecord) {
            throw new Error('No active occupant record');
          }
          return this.resourceShelterService.checkOutShelter(activeRecord.recordId);
        }),
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage = 'Occupancy updated successfully.';
          this.errorMessage = '';
          this.loadShelters();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.errorMessage = this.extractErrorMessage(
            err,
            'Unable to check out occupant.',
          );
        },
      });
  }

  displayOccupancy(item: ShelterEntity): number {
    const value = item.currentOccupancy;
    if (value < 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    if (value > item.capacity) {
      return item.capacity;
    }
    return value;
  }

  availabilityStatus(item: ShelterEntity): 'AVAILABLE' | 'FULL' {
    if (this.displayOccupancy(item) >= item.capacity) {
      return 'FULL';
    }
    return 'AVAILABLE';
  }

  onSearchChange(): void {
    this.currentPage = 1;
    this.normalizePage();
  }

  onPageSizeChange(value: string): void {
    const parsed = Number(value);
    if (Number.isNaN(parsed) || parsed <= 0) {
      return;
    }

    this.pageSize = parsed;
    this.currentPage = 1;
    this.normalizePage();
  }

  prevPage(): void {
    if (this.currentPage <= 1) {
      return;
    }
    this.currentPage -= 1;
    this.normalizePage();
  }

  nextPage(): void {
    if (this.currentPage >= this.totalPages) {
      return;
    }
    this.currentPage += 1;
    this.normalizePage();
  }

  exportSheltersExcel(): void {
    const rows = this.filteredShelters.map((item, index) => {
      const values = [
        index + 1,
        item.name,
        this.managerIdOf(item),
        item.address,
        item.locationUrl,
        item.capacity,
        this.displayOccupancy(item),
        this.availabilityStatus(item),
        this.shelterTypeLabel(item),
      ];

      return `<tr>${values
        .map((value) => `<td>${this.escapeHtml(String(value ?? ''))}</td>`)
        .join('')}</tr>`;
    });

    const dateLabel = new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR');
    const html = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <style>
    body { font-family: Calibri, Arial, sans-serif; margin: 18px; color: #1f2937; }
    h2 { margin: 0 0 6px 0; color: #1d4ed8; }
    .meta { margin: 0 0 14px 0; font-size: 12px; color: #6b7280; }
    table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; }
    thead th {
      background: #1d4ed8;
      color: #ffffff;
      font-size: 12px;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.04em;
      border: 1px solid #93c5fd;
      padding: 9px;
      text-align: left;
    }
    tbody td {
      border: 1px solid #dbeafe;
      padding: 8px;
      font-size: 12px;
      vertical-align: top;
    }
    tbody tr:nth-child(even) { background: #eff6ff; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <h2>BackOffice Shelters Export</h2>
  <p class="meta">Generated on ${this.escapeHtml(dateLabel)} • Total shelters: ${rows.length}</p>
  <table>
    <thead>
      <tr>
        <th>#</th>
        <th>Name</th>
        <th>Manager ID</th>
        <th>Address</th>
        <th>Location URL</th>
        <th>Capacity</th>
        <th>Occupancy</th>
        <th>Status</th>
        <th>Type</th>
      </tr>
    </thead>
    <tbody>
      ${rows.length > 0 ? rows.join('') : '<tr><td colspan="9">No shelters found.</td></tr>'}
    </tbody>
  </table>
</body>
</html>`;

    const blob = new Blob([html], {
      type: 'application/vnd.ms-excel;charset=utf-8;',
    });
    const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const anchor = document.createElement('a');
    const stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    anchor.href = objectUrl;
    anchor.download = `shelters-export-${stamp}.xls`;
    anchor.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
  }

  amenityList(item: ShelterEntity): string {
    if (!item.amenities || item.amenities.length === 0) {
      return '-';
    }

    const values = item.amenities
      .map((a) => {
        if (a && a.amenity) {
          return a.amenity;
        }
        return '';
      })
      .filter((a) => a.length > 0);

    if (values.length === 0) {
      return '-';
    }

    return values.join(', ');
  }

  shelterTypeLabel(item: ShelterEntity): UiShelterType {
    return this.extractShelterType(item);
  }

  private occupantUserId(item: ShelterEntity): number {
    const manager = this.managerIdOf(item);
    const parsed = Number(manager);
    if (!Number.isNaN(parsed) && parsed > 0) {
      return parsed;
    }
    return 1;
  }

  managerIdOf(item: ShelterEntity): string {
    const candidate = item as ShelterEntity & {
      managerId?: number;
      manager?: { userId?: number; id?: number };
    };

    if (typeof candidate.managerId === 'number') {
      return String(candidate.managerId);
    }

    if (candidate.manager && typeof candidate.manager.userId === 'number') {
      return String(candidate.manager.userId);
    }

    if (candidate.manager && typeof candidate.manager.id === 'number') {
      return String(candidate.manager.id);
    }

    return '-';
  }

  mapsUrl(item: ShelterEntity): string {
    const raw = item.locationUrl.trim();
    if (!raw) {
      return 'https://maps.google.com';
    }

    if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(raw)) {
      return raw;
    }

    return `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(raw)}`;
  }

  private latestActiveOccupant(
    records: ShelterOccupantEntity[],
  ): ShelterOccupantEntity | null {
    const active = records.filter((record) => !record.checkOutAt);
    if (active.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    active.sort((a, b) => {
      const aTime = new Date(a.checkInAt).getTime();
      const bTime = new Date(b.checkInAt).getTime();
      return bTime - aTime;
    });

    return active[0];
  }

  private extractShelterType(item: ShelterEntity): UiShelterType {
    const typeAmenity = item.amenities?.find((a) =>
      (a.amenity ?? '').startsWith('TYPE:'),
    );
    const rawType = (typeAmenity?.amenity ?? '').replace('TYPE:', '').toUpperCase();
    if (rawType === 'MEDICAL') {
      return 'MEDICAL';
    }
    return 'LOCAL';
  }

  private toApiStatus(status: UiShelterStatus): ShelterStatus {
    return this.uiToApiStatusMap[status];
  }

  private toUiStatus(status: ShelterStatus): UiShelterStatus {
    return this.apiToUiStatusMap[status];
  }

  private afterMutation(): void {
    this.isSaving = false;
    this.cancelEdit();
    this.loadShelters();
  }

  private normalizePage(): void {
    if (this.currentPage < 1) {
      this.currentPage = 1;
    }

    const max = this.totalPages;
    if (this.currentPage > max) {
      this.currentPage = max;
    }
  }

  private resolveManagerId(requestedId: number): Observable<number> {
    const userId = Number(localStorage.getItem('userId'));
    return of(requestedId > 0 ? requestedId : (userId > 0 ? userId : 1));
  }

  private extractErrorMessage(err: unknown, fallback: string): string {
    if (typeof err === 'object' && err !== null) {
      const withError = err as { error?: { message?: string } };
      if (withError.error && withError.error.message) {
        return withError.error.message;
      }
    }
    return fallback;
  }

  private escapeHtml(value: string): string {
    return value
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
  }
}

type UiShelterStatus = 'AVAILABLE' | 'FULL';
type UiShelterType = 'LOCAL' | 'MEDICAL';

import { Component, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormBuilder, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { switchMap } from 'rxjs';
import { AuthenticatedLayoutComponent } from '../../../layouts/authenticated-layout/authenticated-layout.component';
import { ReviewsTrustService } from '../../../core/services/reviews-trust/reviews-trust.service';
import { ReviewEntity } from '../../../shared/models/reviews/reviews-trust.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-reports',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ReactiveFormsModule, RouterModule, AuthenticatedLayoutComponent],
  templateUrl: './reports.component.html',
  styleUrl: './reports.component.scss',
})
export class ReportsComponent {
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);

  reviews: ReviewEntity[] = [];
  isLoading = false;
  isSubmitting = false;
  successMessage = '';
  errorMessage = '';
  selectedReviewId: number | null = null;

  readonly form = this.fb.nonNullable.group({
    reporterId: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    reviewId: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    reason: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(5)]],
    description: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(10)]],
  });

  constructor(private readonly reviewsTrustService: ReviewsTrustService) {
    this.loadReviews();
  }

  loadReviews(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';

    this.reviewsTrustService.getAllReviews().subscribe({
      next: (items) => {
        this.reviews = items;
        if (items.length > 0) {
          this.selectReview(items[0]);
        } else {
          this.selectedReviewId = null;
        }
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Unable to load reviews for moderation.';
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
    });
  }

  submitReport(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) {
      this.form.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.isSubmitting = true;
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.errorMessage = '';

    const raw = this.form.getRawValue();
    const reviewId = Number(raw.reviewId);

    this.reviewsTrustService
      .moderateReview(reviewId, 'FLAGGED')
      .pipe(
        switchMap(() =>
          this.reviewsTrustService.createModerationAction({
            adminId: Number(raw.reporterId),
            targetType: 'REVIEW',
            targetId: reviewId,
            action: 'WARN',
            reason: `${raw.reason.trim()} | ${raw.description.trim()}`,
          }),
        ),
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage =
            'Report submitted. The selected review has been flagged for moderation.';
          this.isSubmitting = false;
          this.form.patchValue({ reason: '', description: '' });
          this.loadReviews();
        },
        error: () => {
          this.errorMessage =
            'Unable to submit this report right now.';
          this.isSubmitting = false;
        },
      });
  }

  selectReview(review: ReviewEntity): void {
    this.selectedReviewId = review.reviewId;
    this.form.patchValue({
      reporterId: review.authorId,
      reviewId: review.reviewId,
    });
  }

  shortBody(review: ReviewEntity): string {
    if (review.body.length <= 100) {
      return review.body;
    }
    return `${review.body.slice(0, 100)}...`;
  }
}

import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { AlertService } from '../../core/services/alert.service';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/services/auth.service';

import { MapViewComponent } from '../../shared/components/map-view/map-view.component';

const ADMIN_ROLES = ['PLATFORM_ADMIN', 'ORG_ADMIN', 'AGENCY_ADMIN', 'ADMIN'];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-alert-detail',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterModule, MapViewComponent, StatusBadgeComponent],
  template: `
    <div class="p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">

      <!-- ── CREATE MODE ────────────────────────────────────── -->
      <ng-container *ngIf="isNew">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-3">

              </div>

              <label class="text-xs font-bold text-on-surface-variant uppercase tracking-wide">Disaster Type</label>

                    class="flex-1 bg-primary text-on-primary py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm">Save Changes</button>
            <button (click)="cancelEdit()"
                    class="px-5 py-3 rounded-xl border border-white/20 font-bold text-sm">Cancel</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </ng-container>
    </div>
  `

export class AlertDetailComponent implements OnInit {
  alert?: Alert;
  isNew    = false;
  editMode = false;
  basePath = '/alerts'; // set dynamically based on role

  disasterTypes = ['FLOOD', 'EARTHQUAKE', 'WILDFIRE', 'LANDSLIDE', 'STORM', 'CHEMICAL'];
  severities    = ['LOW', 'MODERATE', 'HIGH', 'CRITICAL'];

  formData: any = { title: '', disasterType: 'FLOOD', severity: 'MODERATE', description: '', confidenceScore: null, issuedBy: 1 };
  editData: any = {};

  get mapGeometries() {
    const geoms: any[] = [];
    if (this.alert?.affectedArea)    geoms.push({ type: 'Polygon', wkt: this.alert.affectedArea,    color: '#ff3388' });
    if (this.alert?.predictedArea2h) geoms.push({ type: 'Polygon', wkt: this.alert.predictedArea2h, color: '#ffaa33' });
    if (this.alert?.predictedArea6h) geoms.push({ type: 'Polygon', wkt: this.alert.predictedArea6h, color: '#33aaff' });
    return geoms;
  }

  constructor(
    private route: ActivatedRoute,
    private router: Router,
    private alertService: AlertService,
    private authService: AuthService
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const role = (this.authService.getRole() || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const isAdmin = ['PLATFORM_ADMIN', 'ORG_ADMIN', 'AGENCY_ADMIN', 'ADMIN'].includes(role);
    this.basePath = isAdmin ? '/admin/alerts' : '/alerts';

    // Detect "new" by route param — never rely on window.location
    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    this.isNew = !idParam || idParam === 'new';

    if (!this.isNew) {
      const id = Number(idParam);
      if (!isNaN(id) && id > 0) {
        this.alertService.getAlertById(id).subscribe(data => {
          this.alert    = data;
          this.editData = { ...data };
        });
      }
    }
  }

  createAlert(): void {
    this.alertService.createAlert(this.formData)
      .subscribe(() => this.router.navigate([this.basePath]));
  }
  enableEdit(): void  { this.editMode = true; this.editData = { ...this.alert }; }
  cancelEdit(): void  { this.editMode = false; }
  updateAlert(): void {
    if (!this.alert) return;
    this.alertService.updateAlert(this.alert.id, this.editData)
      .subscribe(u => { this.alert = u; this.editMode = false; });
  }
  deleteAlert(): void {
    if (!this.alert) return;
    if (confirm('Delete this alert?')) {
      this.alertService.deleteAlert(this.alert.id)
        .subscribe({ next: () => this.router.navigate([this.basePath]) });
    }
  }
  resolve(): void {
    if (this.alert) this.alertService.resolveAlert(this.alert.id).subscribe(u => this.alert = u);
  }
  severityClass(s: string): string {
    return ({ CRITICAL: 'bg-error text-white', HIGH: 'bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-400', MODERATE: 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400', LOW: 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400' } as Record<string,string>)[s] ?? '';
  }
}

@Component({

  template: `

export class AlertListComponent implements OnInit {
  alerts: Alert[]   = [];
  filtered: Alert[] = [];
  searchTerm      = '';
  severityFilter  = '';
  statusFilter    = '';
  isAdmin         = false;
  basePath        = '/alerts'; // dynamically set based on role

  constructor(
    private alertService: AlertService,
    private authService: AuthService
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const role = (this.authService.getRole() || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    this.isAdmin = ADMIN_ROLES.includes(role);
    this.basePath = this.isAdmin ? '/admin/alerts' : '/alerts';

    this.alertService.getAllAlerts().subscribe(data => {
      this.alerts   = data;
      this.filtered = data;
    });
  }

  applyFilters(): void {
    this.filtered = this.alerts.filter(a => {
      const matchSearch   = !this.searchTerm ||
        (a.title || '').toLowerCase().includes(this.searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
        a.disasterType.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchTerm.toLowerCase());
      const matchSeverity = !this.severityFilter || a.severity === this.severityFilter;
      const matchStatus   = !this.statusFilter   || a.status   === this.statusFilter;
      return matchSearch && matchSeverity && matchStatus;
    });
  }

  rowClass(severity: string): string {
    return severity === 'CRITICAL' ? 'border-l-2 border-l-error' :
      severity === 'HIGH'     ? 'border-l-2 border-l-orange-500' : '';
  }

  severityClass(severity: string): string {
    return ({
      CRITICAL: 'bg-error text-white',
      HIGH:     'bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-400 border border-orange-500/30',
      MODERATE: 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30',
      LOW:      'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border border-green-500/30',
    } as Record<string, string>)[severity] ?? 'bg-white/10 text-white';
  }

  disasterIcon(type: string): string {
    const m: Record<string, string> = {
      FLOOD: 'water', EARTHQUAKE: 'earthquake', WILDFIRE: 'local_fire_department',
      LANDSLIDE: 'landslide', STORM: 'thunderstorm', CHEMICAL: 'science'
    };
    return m[type] ?? 'emergency';
  }
  disasterIconBg(type: string): string {
    const m: Record<string, string> = {
      FLOOD: 'bg-blue-500/20', EARTHQUAKE: 'bg-orange-500/20', WILDFIRE: 'bg-red-500/20',
      LANDSLIDE: 'bg-amber-500/20', STORM: 'bg-sky-500/20', CHEMICAL: 'bg-purple-500/20'
    };
    return m[type] ?? 'bg-white/10';
  }
  disasterIconColor(type: string): string {
    const m: Record<string, string> = {
      FLOOD: 'text-blue-400', EARTHQUAKE: 'text-orange-400', WILDFIRE: 'text-red-400',
      LANDSLIDE: 'text-amber-400', STORM: 'text-sky-400', CHEMICAL: 'text-purple-400'
    };
    return m[type] ?? 'text-white';
  }
}

=== FILE: \src\app\pages\coordination\help-request-form\help-request-form.component.ts ===
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  FormBuilder,
  FormGroup,
  Validators,
} from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { CoordinationService } from '../../../core/services/coordination.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-help-request-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ReactiveFormsModule, RouterModule],
  templateUrl: './help-request-form.component.html',
})
export class HelpRequestFormComponent implements OnInit {
  form!: FormGroup;
  isLoading = false;
  successMessage = '';
  errorMessage = '';

  requestTypes = [
    'RESCUE',
    'MEDICAL',
    'EVACUATION',
    'SUPPLY',
    'SHELTER',
    'TECHNICAL',
  ];

  constructor(
    private fb: FormBuilder,
    private svc: CoordinationService,
    private router: Router,
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.form = this.fb.group({
      type: ['', Validators.required],
      urgency: [3, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1), Validators.max(5)]],
      location: ['', Validators.required],
      description: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(10)]],
    });
  }

  useGPS(): void {
    navigator.geolocation?.getCurrentPosition((p) =>
      this.form.patchValue({
        location: `${p.coords.latitude.toFixed(4)}, ${p.coords.longitude.toFixed(4)}`,
      }),
    );
  }

  onSubmit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) {
      this.form.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.svc.createRequest(this.form.value).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.successMessage = 'Help request submitted.';
        setTimeout(() => this.router.navigate(['/coordination']), 1200);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.errorMessage = err?.error?.message || 'Submission failed.';
      },
    });
  }

  inv(f: string): boolean {
    const c = this.form.get(f);
    return !!(c?.invalid && (c.dirty || c.touched));
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit, Input } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import {ReactiveFormsModule, FormBuilder, FormGroup, Validators, FormsModule} from '@angular/forms';
import { CoordinationService, HelpRequest, TaskAssignment,
  CoordinationStats, VolunteerMatch } from '../../../core/services/coordination.service';
import { StatusBadgeComponent } from '../../../shared/components/status-badge/status-badge.component';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-help-request-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule, ReactiveFormsModule, StatusBadgeComponent, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './help-request-list.component.html'
})
export class HelpRequestListComponent implements OnInit {

  /** ADMIN sees assign + delete; VOLUNTEER only sees claim */
  @Input() role: string = 'VOLUNTEER';

  requests: HelpRequest[] = [];
  filtered: HelpRequest[] = [];
  isLoading = true;
  errorMessage = '';
  activeFilter = 'ALL';
  expandedId: number | null = null;
  assignmentsMap: Record<number, TaskAssignment[]> = {};
  assignForm!: FormGroup;
  assigningTo: number | null = null;
  matchesMap: Record<number, VolunteerMatch[]> = {};
  loadingMatches: Record<number, boolean> = {};
  stats: CoordinationStats | null = null;

  // Completion
  completingAssignmentId: number | null = null;
  completingForRequestId: number | null = null;
  completionNote = '';
  completionRating = 5;

  filters = ['ALL','OPEN','ASSIGNED','IN_PROGRESS','RESOLVED','CANCELLED'];

  typeIcons: Record<string,string> = {
    RESCUE: 'sos', MEDICAL: 'medical_services',
    EVACUATION: 'directions_run', SUPPLY: 'inventory_2',
    SHELTER: 'home', TECHNICAL: 'build'
  };

  urgencyColors = ['','text-secondary','text-secondary','text-amber-400','text-orange-400','text-error'];

  constructor(private fb: FormBuilder, private svc: CoordinationService) {}

  get isAdmin(): boolean { return this.role === 'ADMIN'; }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.assignForm = this.fb.group({
      responderId: [null, Validators.required],
      notes: ['']
    });
    this.svc.getStats().subscribe({ next: s => this.stats = s });
    this.load();
  }

  load(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.svc.getAllRequests().subscribe({
      next: data => { this.requests = data; this.applyFilter(this.activeFilter); this.isLoading = false; },
      error: () => { this.errorMessage = 'Could not load help requests.'; this.isLoading = false; }
    });
  }

  applyFilter(f: string): void {
    this.activeFilter = f;
    this.filtered = f === 'ALL' ? this.requests : this.requests.filter(r => r.status === f);
  }

  toggleExpand(id: number): void {
    if (this.expandedId === id) { this.expandedId = null; return; }
    this.expandedId = id;
    if (!this.assignmentsMap[id]) {
      this.svc.getAssignmentsByRequest(id).subscribe(a => this.assignmentsMap[id] = a);
    }
  }

  // Admin assign modal
  openAssign(id: number): void {
    this.assigningTo = id;
    this.assignForm.reset();
    this.loadingMatches[id] = true;
    this.svc.getMatches(id).subscribe({
      next: m => { this.matchesMap[id] = m; this.loadingMatches[id] = false; },
      error: () => { this.matchesMap[id] = []; this.loadingMatches[id] = false; }
    });
  }
  closeAssign(): void { this.assigningTo = null; }

  submitAssignment(): void {
    if (this.assignForm.invalid || !this.assigningTo) return;
    this.svc.createAssignment(this.assigningTo, this.assignForm.value).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.svc.getAssignmentsByRequest(this.assigningTo!)
          .subscribe(a => this.assignmentsMap[this.assigningTo!] = a);
        this.closeAssign();
      },
      error: () => this.errorMessage = 'Assignment failed.'
    });
  }

  assignFromMatch(volunteerId: number): void {
    if (!this.assigningTo) return;
    this.svc.createAssignment(this.assigningTo, { responderId: volunteerId }).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.svc.getAssignmentsByRequest(this.assigningTo!)
          .subscribe(a => this.assignmentsMap[this.assigningTo!] = a);
        this.closeAssign();
      }
    });
  }

  updateStatus(id: number, status: string): void {
    this.svc.updateAssignmentStatus(id, status).subscribe(() => {
      const reqId = Object.keys(this.assignmentsMap).find(k =>
        this.assignmentsMap[+k].some(a => a.id === id)
      );
      if (reqId) {
        this.svc.getAssignmentsByRequest(+reqId).subscribe(a => this.assignmentsMap[+reqId] = a);
      }
    });
  }

  // Completion modal
  openComplete(assignmentId: number, requestId: number): void {
    this.completingAssignmentId = assignmentId;
    this.completingForRequestId = requestId;
    this.completionNote = '';
    this.completionRating = 5;
  }
  closeComplete(): void { this.completingAssignmentId = null; this.completingForRequestId = null; }

  submitCompletion(): void {
    if (!this.completingAssignmentId || !this.completingForRequestId) return;
    this.svc.completeAssignment(this.completingAssignmentId, this.completionNote, this.completionRating)
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          const reqId = this.completingForRequestId!;
          this.svc.getAssignmentsByRequest(reqId).subscribe(a => this.assignmentsMap[reqId] = a);
          this.closeComplete();
        }
      });
  }

  getStars(count: number): number[] { return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => i + 1); }
  getTypeIcon(type: string): string { return this.typeIcons[type] || 'help'; }
  getUrgencyColor(u: number): string { return this.urgencyColors[u] || 'text-on-surface-variant'; }
  urgencyDots(u: number): number[] { return Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, i) => i); }
}

import { Component } from '@angular/core';

@Component({

  standalone: true,
  imports: [],
  templateUrl: './task-assignment-list.component.html',
  styleUrl: './task-assignment-list.component.scss'
})
export class TaskAssignmentListComponent {

}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\dashboard\dashboard.component.ts ===
// src/app/pages/dashboard/dashboard.component.ts
import { Component, OnInit, OnDestroy } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterModule, ActivatedRoute } from '@angular/router';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { forkJoin, interval, Subscription } from 'rxjs';
import { startWith, switchMap } from 'rxjs/operators';

import { SensorService } from '../../core/services/sensor.service';
import { AlertService } from '../../core/services/alert.service';
import { EvacuationService } from '../../core/services/evacuation.service';
import { IncidentService, IncidentReport, StatusHistory } from '../../core/services/incident.service';
import { CoordinationService, HelpRequest, TaskAssignment,
  CoordinationStats, VolunteerMatch } from '../../core/services/coordination.service';
import { StatusBadgeComponent } from '../../shared/components/status-badge/status-badge.component';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-dashboard',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule, FormsModule, StatusBadgeComponent],
  templateUrl: './dashboard.component.html'
})
export class DashboardComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {

  // ── Stats from coordination service ──────────────────
  stats: CoordinationStats | null = null;

  // ── Incidents ─────────────────────────────────────────
  incidents: IncidentReport[] = [];
  filteredIncidents: IncidentReport[] = [];
  incidentFilter = 'ALL';
  incidentSearch = '';
  expandedIncidentId: number | null = null;
  historyMap: Record<number, StatusHistory[] | undefined> = {};
  incidentLoading = true;

  // ── Help Requests ─────────────────────────────────────
  requests: HelpRequest[] = [];
  filteredRequests: HelpRequest[] = [];
  requestFilter = 'ALL';
  requestSearch = '';
  expandedRequestId: number | null = null;
  assignmentsMap: Record<number, TaskAssignment[] | undefined> = {};
  requestLoading = true;

  // ── Live sensor/alert/routes data ────────────────────
  totalSensors = 0;
  activeSensors = 0;
  offlineSensors = 0;
  activeAlerts = 0;
  criticalAlerts = 0;
  activeRoutes = 0;
  anomaliesCount = 0;

  // ── Live alerts feed ─────────────────────────────────
  recentAlerts: any[] = [];

  // ── Static dashboard data (kept from original) ───────
  readonly notifications = [
    { color: 'bg-secondary', time: '2 min ago',  message: 'Scheduler: 3 geofence zones expired and were flagged.' },
    { color: 'bg-primary',   time: '8 min ago',  message: 'New evacuation route activated for alert #12.' },
    { color: 'bg-amber-500', time: '15 min ago', message: 'Sensor SEISMIC-04 has gone offline (no ping).' },
    { color: 'bg-error',     time: '1 hr ago',   message: 'CRITICAL alert auto-triggered — Flood Gauge threshold exceeded.' }
  ];

  readonly systemStatus = [
    { label: 'JWT Auth',         status: 'Online',   color: 'text-green-400' },
    { label: 'WebSocket',        status: 'Live',     color: 'text-green-400' },
    { label: 'Cache (Caffeine)', status: 'Active',   color: 'text-green-400' },
    { label: 'Scheduler',        status: 'Running',  color: 'text-green-400' },
    { label: 'PostGIS Spatial',  status: 'Ready',    color: 'text-green-400' }
  ];

  readonly impactBars = [
    { label: 'Civic Engagement', value: 82 },
    { label: 'Crisis Response',  value: 45 },
    { label: 'Social Support',   value: 67 }
  ];

  // ── Quick links ──────────────────────────────────────
  readonly quickLinks = [
    { label: 'Add Sensor',     icon: 'sensors',     route: '/sensors/new',    color: 'text-secondary' },
    { label: 'Create Alert',   icon: 'add_alert',   route: '/alerts/new',     color: 'text-error' },
    { label: 'New Route',      icon: 'add_road',    route: '/evacuation/new', color: 'text-primary' },
    { label: 'Geofence Zones', icon: 'map',         route: '/geofence',       color: 'text-amber-400' }
  ];

  // ── Delete confirm ────────────────────────────────────
  deleteTarget: { type: 'incident' | 'request'; id: number } | null = null;

  // ── Assign modal ──────────────────────────────────────
  assigningToRequestId: number | null = null;
  matchesForRequest: VolunteerMatch[] = [];
  loadingMatches = false;
  assignResponderId: number | null = null;
  assignNotes = '';
  assignError = '';

  // ── Active tab ────────────────────────────────────────
  activeTab: string = 'overview';

  // ── Status/urgency helpers ───────────────────────────
  incidentStatuses = ['ALL', 'PENDING', 'VERIFIED', 'REJECTED', 'ESCALATED'];
  requestStatuses  = ['ALL', 'OPEN', 'ASSIGNED', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'RESOLVED', 'CANCELLED'];

  severityBadge: Record<string, string> = {
    LOW:      'bg-secondary/10 text-secondary border-secondary/20',
    MEDIUM:   'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/20',
    HIGH:     'bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400 border-orange-500/20',
    CRITICAL: 'bg-error/20 text-error border-error/30'
  };

  urgencyColors = ['', 'bg-secondary', 'bg-secondary', 'bg-amber-400', 'bg-orange-400', 'bg-error'];

  // ── Private subscriptions ────────────────────────────
  private liveDataSub?: Subscription;

  // ── Single constructor ────────────────────────────────
  constructor(
    private sensorService: SensorService,
    private alertService: AlertService,
    private evacuationService: EvacuationService,
    private incSvc: IncidentService,
    private coordSvc: CoordinationService,
    private route: ActivatedRoute
  ) {}

  // ── Lifecycle hooks ───────────────────────────────────
  ngOnInit(): void {
    // Read optional tab from route data
    const routeTab = this.route.snapshot.data['tab'];
    if (routeTab && ['overview', 'incidents', 'coordination'].includes(routeTab)) {
      this.activeTab = routeTab;
    }

    // Load data from coordination/incident services
    this.loadStats();
    this.loadIncidents();
    this.loadRequests();

    // Auto-refresh live sensor/alert/routes every 30 seconds
    this.liveDataSub = interval(30_000).pipe(
      startWith(0),
      switchMap(() => forkJoin({
        sensors: this.sensorService.getAllSensors(),
        alerts:  this.alertService.getAllAlerts(),
        routes:  this.evacuationService.getAllRoutes()
      }))
    ).subscribe(({ sensors, alerts, routes }) => {
      this.totalSensors   = sensors.length;
      this.activeSensors  = sensors.filter(s => s.status === 'ACTIVE').length;
      this.offlineSensors = sensors.filter(s => s.status === 'OFFLINE').length;

      this.activeAlerts   = alerts.filter(a => a.status === 'ACTIVE').length;
      this.criticalAlerts = alerts.filter(a => a.severity === 'CRITICAL' && a.status === 'ACTIVE').length;

      this.activeRoutes   = routes.filter(r => r.status === 'ACTIVE').length;

      // Recent 4 alerts sorted newest first
      this.recentAlerts = [...alerts]
        .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.issuedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.issuedAt).getTime())
        .slice(0, 4)
        .map(a => ({
          ...a,
          icon:          this.alertIcon(a.disasterType),
          iconBg:        this.alertIconBg(a.severity),
          severityClass: this.severityClass(a.severity),
          borderClass:   this.borderClass(a.severity)
        }));
    });
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.liveDataSub?.unsubscribe();
  }

  // ── Getter for stat cards (avoid name conflict with `stats`) ──
  get statsCards() {
    return [
      {
        label: 'Active Sensors',
        value: this.activeSensors,
        subLabel: `${this.offlineSensors} offline`,
        icon: 'sensors',
        iconBg: 'bg-secondary/20',
        iconColor: 'text-secondary',
        trend: this.offlineSensors > 0 ? `⚠ ${this.offlineSensors} need attention` : '✓ All sensors online',
        trendColor: this.offlineSensors > 0 ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-green-400'
      },
      {
        label: 'Active Alerts',
        value: this.activeAlerts,
        subLabel: `${this.criticalAlerts} critical`,
        icon: 'emergency',
        iconBg: 'bg-error/20',
        iconColor: 'text-error',
        trend: this.criticalAlerts > 0 ? `🔴 ${this.criticalAlerts} critical active` : '✓ No critical alerts',
        trendColor: this.criticalAlerts > 0 ? 'text-error' : 'text-green-400'
      },
      {
        label: 'Active Routes',
        value: this.activeRoutes,
        subLabel: 'evacuation routes',
        icon: 'directions',
        iconBg: 'bg-primary/20',
        iconColor: 'text-primary',
        trend: this.activeRoutes > 0 ? `${this.activeRoutes} route(s) operational` : 'No active evacuations',
        trendColor: this.activeRoutes > 0 ? 'text-primary' : 'text-on-surface-variant'
      },
      {
        label: 'Anomalies',
        value: this.anomaliesCount,
        subLabel: 'detected readings',
        icon: 'troubleshoot',
        iconBg: 'bg-amber-500/20',
        iconColor: 'text-amber-400',
        trend: this.anomaliesCount > 0 ? `${this.anomaliesCount} anomalous readings` : '✓ All readings normal',
        trendColor: this.anomaliesCount > 0 ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-green-400'
      }
    ];
  }

  // ── Data loading methods ──────────────────────────────
  loadStats(): void {
    this.coordSvc.getStats().subscribe({ next: s => this.stats = s });
  }

  loadIncidents(): void {
    this.incidentLoading = true;
    this.incSvc.getAll().subscribe({
      next: d => { this.incidents = d; this.applyIncidentFilters(); this.incidentLoading = false; },
      error: () => this.incidentLoading = false
    });
  }

  loadRequests(): void {
    this.requestLoading = true;
    this.coordSvc.getAllRequests().subscribe({
      next: d => { this.requests = d; this.applyRequestFilters(); this.requestLoading = false; },
      error: () => this.requestLoading = false
    });
  }

  // ── Filter methods ────────────────────────────────────
  applyIncidentFilters(): void {
    this.filteredIncidents = this.incidents.filter(r => {
      const statusMatch = this.incidentFilter === 'ALL' || r.status === this.incidentFilter;
      const searchMatch = !this.incidentSearch ||
        r.disasterType?.toLowerCase().includes(this.incidentSearch.toLowerCase()) ||
        r.location?.toLowerCase().includes(this.incidentSearch.toLowerCase());
      return statusMatch && searchMatch;
    });
  }

  applyRequestFilters(): void {
    this.filteredRequests = this.requests.filter(r => {
      const statusMatch = this.requestFilter === 'ALL' || r.status === this.requestFilter;
      const searchMatch = !this.requestSearch ||
        r.type?.toLowerCase().includes(this.requestSearch.toLowerCase()) ||
        r.location?.toLowerCase().includes(this.requestSearch.toLowerCase());
      return statusMatch && searchMatch;
    });
  }

  // ── Incident actions ──────────────────────────────────
  updateIncidentStatus(id: number, status: string): void {
    this.incSvc.updateStatus(id, status).subscribe({
      next: updated => {
        const idx = this.incidents.findIndex(x => x.id === id);
        if (idx !== -1) this.incidents[idx] = updated as IncidentReport;
        this.applyIncidentFilters();
        this.incSvc.getHistory(id).subscribe(h => this.historyMap[id] = h);
      }
    });
  }

  toggleIncident(id: number): void {
    this.expandedIncidentId = this.expandedIncidentId === id ? null : id;
    if (this.expandedIncidentId === id && !this.historyMap[id]) {
      this.incSvc.getHistory(id).subscribe(h => this.historyMap[id] = h);
    }
  }

  // ── Request actions ───────────────────────────────────
  updateRequestStatus(id: number, status: string): void {
    const req = this.requests.find(x => x.id === id);
    if (!req) return;
    this.coordSvc.updateRequest(id, { ...req, status }).subscribe({
      next: updated => {
        const idx = this.requests.findIndex(x => x.id === id);
        if (idx !== -1) this.requests[idx] = updated;
        this.applyRequestFilters();
      }
    });
  }

  toggleRequest(id: number): void {
    this.expandedRequestId = this.expandedRequestId === id ? null : id;
    if (this.expandedRequestId === id && !this.assignmentsMap[id]) {
      this.coordSvc.getAssignmentsByRequest(id).subscribe(a => this.assignmentsMap[id] = a);
    }
  }

  updateAssignmentStatus(id: number, status: string, reqId: number): void {
    this.coordSvc.updateAssignmentStatus(id, status).subscribe(() => {
      this.coordSvc.getAssignmentsByRequest(reqId).subscribe(a => this.assignmentsMap[reqId] = a);
    });
  }

  // ── Assign modal ──────────────────────────────────────
  openAssignModal(requestId: number, event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
    this.assigningToRequestId = requestId;
    this.assignResponderId = null;
    this.assignNotes = '';
    this.assignError = '';
    this.loadingMatches = true;
    this.matchesForRequest = [];
    this.coordSvc.getMatches(requestId).subscribe({
      next: m => { this.matchesForRequest = m; this.loadingMatches = false; },
      error: () => { this.matchesForRequest = []; this.loadingMatches = false; }
    });
  }

  closeAssignModal(): void {
    this.assigningToRequestId = null;
  }

  submitAssignment(): void {
    if (!this.assigningToRequestId || !this.assignResponderId) {
      this.assignError = 'Please enter a responder ID.';
      return;
    }
    this.coordSvc.createAssignment(
      this.assigningToRequestId,
      { responderId: this.assignResponderId, notes: this.assignNotes }
    ).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        const reqId = this.assigningToRequestId!;
        this.coordSvc.getAssignmentsByRequest(reqId).subscribe(a => this.assignmentsMap[reqId] = a);
        this.closeAssignModal();
      },
      error: () => this.assignError = 'Assignment failed.'
    });
  }

  assignFromMatch(volunteerId: number): void {
    if (!this.assigningToRequestId) return;
    this.coordSvc.createAssignment(
      this.assigningToRequestId,
      { responderId: volunteerId }
    ).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        const reqId = this.assigningToRequestId!;
        this.coordSvc.getAssignmentsByRequest(reqId).subscribe(a => this.assignmentsMap[reqId] = a);
        this.closeAssignModal();
      },
      error: () => this.assignError = 'Assignment failed.'
    });
  }

  // ── Delete ────────────────────────────────────────────
  confirmDelete(type: 'incident' | 'request', id: number): void {
    this.deleteTarget = { type, id };
  }

  doDelete(): void {
    if (!this.deleteTarget) return;
    if (this.deleteTarget.type === 'incident') {
      this.incSvc.delete(this.deleteTarget.id).subscribe({
        next: () => { this.deleteTarget = null; this.loadIncidents(); }
      });
    } else {
      this.coordSvc.deleteRequest(this.deleteTarget.id).subscribe({
        next: () => { this.deleteTarget = null; this.loadRequests(); }
      });
    }
  }

  // ── Template helpers ──────────────────────────────────
  credColor(score?: number): string {
    if (!score) return 'text-on-surface-variant';
    return score >= 0.8 ? 'text-secondary' : score >= 0.5 ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-error';
  }

  getUrgencyDotClass(urgency: number, dotIndex: number): string {
    return dotIndex < urgency ? this.urgencyColors[urgency] || 'bg-on-surface-variant' : 'bg-white/10';
  }

  getPendingIncidentCount(): number {
    return this.incidents.filter(i => i.status === 'PENDING').length;
  }

  getOpenRequestCount(): number {
    return this.requests.filter(r => r.status === 'OPEN').length;
  }

  getTimelineIcon(status: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      PENDING: 'schedule', VERIFIED: 'check', REJECTED: 'close', ESCALATED: 'arrow_upward'
    };
    return map[status] || 'circle';
  }

  getTimelineDotStyle(status: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      PENDING:   'background:rgba(245,158,11,0.8)',
      VERIFIED:  'background:rgba(105,156,255,0.8)',
      REJECTED:  'background:rgba(255,110,132,0.8)',
      ESCALATED: 'background:rgba(249,115,22,0.8)'
    };
    return map[status] || 'background:rgba(255,255,255,0.2)';
  }

  getStatusPill(status: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      PENDING:   'background:rgba(245,158,11,0.15);color:#fbbf24',
      VERIFIED:  'background:rgba(105,156,255,0.15);color:#699cff',
      REJECTED:  'background:rgba(255,110,132,0.15);color:#ff6e84',
      ESCALATED: 'background:rgba(249,115,22,0.15);color:#fb923c'
    };
    return map[status] || 'background:rgba(255,255,255,0.05);color:#94a3b8';
  }

  // ── Alert icons and styling ───────────────────────────
  alertIcon(type: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      FLOOD: 'water', EARTHQUAKE: 'earthquake', WILDFIRE: 'local_fire_department',
      LANDSLIDE: 'landslide', STORM: 'thunderstorm', CHEMICAL: 'science'
    };
    return map[type] ?? 'emergency';
  }

  alertIconBg(severity: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      CRITICAL: 'bg-error/20 text-error',
      HIGH: 'bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-400',
      MODERATE: 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400',
      LOW: 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400'
    };
    return map[severity] ?? 'bg-white/10 text-white';
  }

  severityClass(severity: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      CRITICAL: 'bg-error text-white',
      HIGH: 'bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-400 border border-orange-500/30',
      MODERATE: 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400',
      LOW: 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400'
    };
    return map[severity] ?? '';
  }

  borderClass(severity: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      CRITICAL: 'border-error',
      HIGH: 'border-orange-500',
      MODERATE: 'border-amber-500',
      LOW: 'border-green-500'
    };
    return map[severity] ?? 'border-white/10';
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit, AfterViewInit, OnDestroy, ElementRef, ViewChild, ChangeDetectorRef } from '@angular/core';

@Component({

          Pin your <span class="text-amber-400 font-bold">origin</span> and <span class="text-green-400 font-bold">shelter</span> on the map below. The road route will be computed automatically via OSRM.

                    class="w-full bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-primary">
              <option [ngValue]="null" disabled>Select an alert...</option>

                {{ a.title || ('Alert #' + a.id) }} — {{ a.disasterType }} · {{ a.severity }}

          </div>
          <div>

                   class="w-full bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-primary" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-on-surface-variant mb-2">Capacity (people)</label>
            <input type="number" [(ngModel)]="newRoute.capacity" placeholder="500"

          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Pin mode selector -->

                  class="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-all"

            <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-sm align-middle mr-1">my_location</span>
            {{ newRoute.fromLatitude ? ('Origin set (' + (newRoute.fromLatitude|number:\'1.4-4\') + ', ' + (newRoute.fromLongitude|number:\'1.4-4\') + ')') : 'Click map to set Origin' }}

            <span class="bg-black/75 text-white text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full">
              {{ pinMode === 'origin' ? '🟡 Click to place Origin point' : '🟢 Click to place Shelter point' }}
            </span>

          <!-- Mini map -->

          <div class="p-5">

              </div>

                <p class="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest text-on-surface-variant mb-0.5">Capacity</p>
                <p class="text-base font-black">{{ r.capacity ?? '—' }}<span class="text-[10px] font-normal ml-0.5">ppl</span></p>

              <a [routerLink]="['/evacuation', r.id]"

                Details

              <button *ngIf="r.status === 'PLANNED'" (click)="activate(r)"

        <p class="font-bold">No routes found</p>
        <p class="text-sm mt-1">Try changing your filter or create a new route above</p>
