import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { ReactiveFormsModule, FormBuilder, FormGroup, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { CoordinationService } from '../../../core/services/coordination.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-help-portal',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ReactiveFormsModule, RouterModule],
  templateUrl: './help-portal.component.html'
})
export class HelpPortalComponent implements OnInit {

  step: 'select' | 'form' | 'submitted' = 'select';
  selectedNeed: any = null;
  isLoading = false;
  errorMessage = '';
  submittedId: number | null = null;
  form!: FormGroup;

  needs = [
    {
      type: 'RESCUE',
      label: 'I need rescue',
      icon: 'sos',
      color: 'text-error',
      bg: 'bg-error',
      cardBg: 'border-error/40 bg-error/10',
      glow: 'shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(255,110,132,0.3)]',
      desc: 'Trapped, stranded, or in immediate danger',
      urgency: 5
    },
    {
      type: 'MEDICAL',
      label: 'Medical help',
      icon: 'medical_services',
      color: 'text-red-300',
      bg: 'bg-red-500',
      cardBg: 'border-red-400/40 bg-red-400/10',
      glow: 'shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(239,68,68,0.3)]',
      desc: 'Injury, illness, or someone needs first aid',
      urgency: 5
    },
    {
      type: 'EVACUATION',
      label: 'Need evacuation',
      icon: 'directions_run',
      color: 'text-orange-400',
      bg: 'bg-orange-500',
      cardBg: 'border-orange-400/40 bg-orange-400/10',
      glow: 'shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(249,115,22,0.25)]',
      desc: 'Need transport to leave a dangerous area',
      urgency: 4
    },
    {
      type: 'SUPPLY',
      label: 'Food & Supplies',
      icon: 'inventory_2',
      color: 'text-amber-400',
      bg: 'bg-amber-500',
      cardBg: 'border-amber-400/40 bg-amber-400/10',
      glow: 'shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(245,158,11,0.2)]',
      desc: 'Food, water, medicine, or essentials',
      urgency: 3
    },
    {
      type: 'SHELTER',
      label: 'Need shelter',
      icon: 'home',
      color: 'text-secondary',
      bg: 'bg-secondary',
      cardBg: 'border-secondary/40 bg-secondary/10',
      glow: 'shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(105,156,255,0.2)]',
      desc: 'Need a safe place to stay',
      urgency: 4
    },
    {
      type: 'TECHNICAL',
      label: 'Technical help',
      icon: 'build',
      color: 'text-primary',
      bg: 'bg-primary',
      cardBg: 'border-primary/40 bg-primary/10',
      glow: 'shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(172,163,255,0.2)]',
      desc: 'Power, communications, or infrastructure',
      urgency: 2
    },
  ];

  constructor(private fb: FormBuilder, private svc: CoordinationService) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.form = this.fb.group({
      type:        ['', Validators.required],
      urgency:     [3, Validators.required],
      location:    ['', Validators.required],
      description: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(10)]],
    });
  }

  selectNeed(need: any): void {
    this.selectedNeed = need;
    this.form.patchValue({ type: need.type, urgency: need.urgency });
    this.step = 'form';
  }

  useGPS(): void {
    navigator.geolocation?.getCurrentPosition(p =>
      this.form.patchValue({ location: `${p.coords.latitude.toFixed(4)}, ${p.coords.longitude.toFixed(4)}` })
    );
  }

  submit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) { this.form.markAllAsTouched(); return; }
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.svc.createRequest(this.form.value).subscribe({
      next: r => { this.isLoading = false; this.submittedId = r.id!; this.step = 'submitted'; },
      error: err => { this.isLoading = false; this.errorMessage = err?.error?.message || 'Submission failed.'; }
    });
  }

  inv(f: string): boolean {
    const c = this.form.get(f);
    return !!(c?.invalid && (c.dirty || c.touched));
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\public\home\home.component.ts ===
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { IncidentService, IncidentReport } from '../../../core/services/incident.service';
import { CoordinationService, CoordinationStats } from '../../../core/services/coordination.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-home',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule],
  templateUrl: './home.component.html'
})
export class HomeComponent implements OnInit {

  incidents: IncidentReport[] = [];
  stats: CoordinationStats | null = null;
  isLoading = true;

  quickActions = [
    {
      label: 'Report an Incident',
      icon: 'report_problem',
      route: '/report',
      desc: 'Flood, fire, earthquake or other emergency',
      color: 'text-error',
      border: 'border-error/30',
      bg: 'bg-error/10',
      glow: 'hover:shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(255,110,132,0.2)]',
      btnBg: 'bg-error',
      btnText: 'text-on-error'
    },
    {
      label: 'Request Help',
      icon: 'sos',
      route: '/help',
      desc: 'Rescue, medical, evacuation or supplies',
      color: 'text-orange-400',
      border: 'border-orange-400/30',
      bg: 'bg-orange-400/10',
      glow: 'hover:shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(251,146,60,0.2)]',
      btnBg: 'bg-orange-500',
      btnText: 'text-white'
    },
    {
      label: 'Volunteer on a Mission',
      icon: 'explore',
      route: '/missions',
      desc: 'Find volunteer opportunities near you',
      color: 'text-secondary',
      border: 'border-secondary/30',
      bg: 'bg-secondary/10',
      glow: 'hover:shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(105,156,255,0.2)]',
      btnBg: 'bg-secondary',
      btnText: 'text-on-secondary'
    },
  ];

  severityConfig: Record<string, { bar: string; dot: string; label: string }> = {
    LOW:      { bar: 'bg-secondary',  dot: 'bg-secondary',  label: 'Low'      },
    MEDIUM:   { bar: 'bg-amber-400',  dot: 'bg-amber-400',  label: 'Medium'   },
    HIGH:     { bar: 'bg-orange-400', dot: 'bg-orange-400', label: 'High'     },
    CRITICAL: { bar: 'bg-error',      dot: 'bg-error',      label: 'Critical' },
  };

  typeIcon: Record<string, string> = {
    FLOOD: 'water', EARTHQUAKE: 'crisis_alert',
    WILDFIRE: 'local_fire_department', HURRICANE: 'storm',
    TSUNAMI: 'waves', DROUGHT: 'wb_sunny'
  };

  emergencyContacts = [
    { name: 'Civil Protection', number: '197', icon: 'emergency', color: 'text-error'      },
    { name: 'SAMU Medical',     number: '190', icon: 'local_hospital', color: 'text-red-400'   },
    { name: 'Fire Brigade',     number: '198', icon: 'local_fire_department', color: 'text-orange-400' },
  ];

  constructor(
    private incSvc: IncidentService,
    private coordSvc: CoordinationService
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.coordSvc.getStats().subscribe({ next: s => this.stats = s });
    this.incSvc.getAll().subscribe({
      next: d => { this.incidents = d.slice(0, 6); this.isLoading = false; },
      error: () => this.isLoading = false
    });
  }

  getSeverity(s?: string) { return this.severityConfig[s || ''] || this.severityConfig['LOW']; }
  getIcon(t: string): string { return this.typeIcon[t] || 'warning'; }

  statusDot(s?: string): string {
    const m: Record<string,string> = {
      PENDING: 'bg-amber-400 animate-pulse', VERIFIED: 'bg-secondary',
      REJECTED: 'bg-error', ESCALATED: 'bg-orange-400'
    };
    return m[s || ''] || 'bg-on-surface-variant';
  }

  timeAgo(dateStr?: string): string {
    if (!dateStr) return '';
    const diff = Date.now() - new Date(dateStr).getTime();
    const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
    if (mins < 60) return `${mins}m ago`;
    const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60);
    if (hrs < 24) return `${hrs}h ago`;
    return `${Math.floor(hrs/24)}d ago`;
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\public\mission-board\mission-board.component.ts ===
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { CoordinationService, HelpRequest, TaskAssignment } from '../../../core/services/coordination.service';

interface MissionCard extends HelpRequest {
  expanded: boolean;
  claiming: boolean;
  claimed: boolean;
  assignmentId?: number;
  urgency: number; // Ensure urgency is always a number
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-mission-board',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './mission-board.component.html'
})
export class MissionBoardComponent implements OnInit {

  missions: MissionCard[] = [];
  filtered: MissionCard[] = [];
  isLoading = true;
  errorMessage = '';
  searchQuery = '';
  activeType = 'ALL';
  activeUrgency = 0;
  showClaimedOnly = false;

  types = ['ALL','RESCUE','MEDICAL','EVACUATION','SUPPLY','SHELTER','TECHNICAL'];

  typeConfig: Record<string, { icon: string; color: string; bg: string; label: string }> = {
    RESCUE:     { icon: 'sos',               color: 'text-error',      bg: 'bg-error/15 border-error/30',      label: 'Rescue'     },
    MEDICAL:    { icon: 'medical_services',  color: 'text-red-300',    bg: 'bg-red-400/15 border-red-400/30',  label: 'Medical'    },
    EVACUATION: { icon: 'directions_run',    color: 'text-orange-400', bg: 'bg-orange-400/15 border-orange-400/30', label: 'Evacuation'},
    SUPPLY:     { icon: 'inventory_2',       color: 'text-amber-400',  bg: 'bg-amber-400/15 border-amber-400/30', label: 'Supply'   },
    SHELTER:    { icon: 'home',              color: 'text-secondary',  bg: 'bg-secondary/15 border-secondary/30',  label: 'Shelter'  },
    TECHNICAL:  { icon: 'build',             color: 'text-primary',    bg: 'bg-primary/15 border-primary/30',  label: 'Technical'  },
  };

  urgencyLabels = ['All urgency','Low','Fair','Moderate','High','Critical'];
  urgencyColors = ['','text-secondary','text-secondary','text-amber-400','text-orange-400','text-error'];

  volunteerId = 1; // TODO: replace with auth user ID

  constructor(private svc: CoordinationService) {}

  ngOnInit(): void { this.load(); }


  load(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.svc.getAllRequests().subscribe({
      next: data => {
        this.missions = data
          .filter(r => r.status === 'OPEN' || r.status === 'ASSIGNED')
          .map(r => ({ ...r, expanded: false, claiming: false, claimed: false, urgency: r.urgency || 0 })); // Ensure urgency is always a number
        this.applyFilters();
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => { this.errorMessage = 'Could not load missions.'; this.isLoading = false; }
    });
  }

  applyFilters(): void {
    this.filtered = this.missions.filter(m => {
      const typeMatch  = this.activeType === 'ALL' || m.type === this.activeType;
      const urgMatch   = this.activeUrgency === 0   || m.urgency >= this.activeUrgency;
      const searchMatch = !this.searchQuery ||
        m.location?.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
        m.description?.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
        m.type?.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchQuery.toLowerCase());
      const claimMatch  = !this.showClaimedOnly || m.claimed;
      return typeMatch && urgMatch && searchMatch && claimMatch;
    });
  }

  claim(mission: MissionCard): void {
    mission.claiming = true;
    this.svc.createAssignment(mission.id!, { responderId: this.volunteerId }).subscribe({
      next: a => {
        mission.claiming = false;
        mission.claimed = true;
        mission.assignmentId = a.id;
      },
      error: () => { mission.claiming = false; this.errorMessage = 'Could not claim mission.'; }
    });
  }

  getType(type: string) {
    return this.typeConfig[type] || { icon: 'help', color: 'text-on-surface-variant', bg: 'bg-white/5 border-white/10', label: type };
  }

  urgencyDots(u: number): number[] { return [0,1,2,3,4]; }

  timeAgo(dateStr?: string): string {
    if (!dateStr) return '';
    const diff = Date.now() - new Date(dateStr).getTime();
    const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
    if (mins < 60) return `${mins}m ago`;
    const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60);
    if (hrs < 24) return `${hrs}h ago`;
    return `${Math.floor(hrs / 24)}d ago`;
  }

  getCriticalCount(): number {
    return this.missions.filter(m => m.urgency >= 4).length;
  }

  getClaimedCount(): number {
    return this.missions.filter(m => m.claimed).length;
  }

  getUrgencyBg(urgency: number): string {
    const map: Record<number, string> = {
      1: 'bg-secondary', 2: 'bg-secondary',
      3: 'bg-amber-400', 4: 'bg-orange-400', 5: 'bg-error'
    };
    return map[urgency] || 'bg-on-surface-variant';
  }

  getClaimBtnClass(type: string): string {
    const t = this.getType(type);
    // convert text-error → bg-error/20, text-secondary → bg-secondary/20 etc
    const bg = t.color
      .replace('text-', 'bg-')
      .replace('-400', '-400/20')
      .replace('-300', '-300/20');
    return `${bg} border border-current hover:opacity-80`;
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\public\report-wizard\report-wizard.component.ts ===
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { ReactiveFormsModule, FormBuilder, FormGroup, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { IncidentService } from '../../../core/services/incident.service';

interface DisasterType {
  value: string;
  label: string;
  icon: string;
  color: string;
  bg: string;
  description: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-report-wizard',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ReactiveFormsModule, RouterModule],
  templateUrl: './report-wizard.component.html'
})
export class ReportWizardComponent implements OnInit {

  currentStep = 1;
  totalSteps = 3;
  isLoading = false;
  submitted = false;
  submittedId: number | null = null;
  errorMessage = '';
  isDragOver = false;

  selectedType: DisasterType | null = null;
  selectedSeverity = '';
  uploadedFiles: Array<{ file: File; preview: string; uploading: boolean; done: boolean }> = [];

  form!: FormGroup;

  disasterTypes: DisasterType[] = [
    { value: 'FLOOD',      label: 'Flood',      icon: 'water',                  color: 'text-blue-400',   bg: 'border-blue-400/40 bg-blue-400/10',   description: 'Rising water, flooded roads or homes'           },
    { value: 'WILDFIRE',   label: 'Wildfire',   icon: 'local_fire_department',  color: 'text-orange-400', bg: 'border-orange-400/40 bg-orange-400/10', description: 'Fire spreading through vegetation or buildings' },
    { value: 'EARTHQUAKE', label: 'Earthquake', icon: 'crisis_alert',           color: 'text-amber-400',  bg: 'border-amber-400/40 bg-amber-400/10', description: 'Ground shaking, structural damage'              },
    { value: 'HURRICANE',  label: 'Storm',      icon: 'storm',                  color: 'text-purple-400', bg: 'border-purple-400/40 bg-purple-400/10', description: 'Severe wind, cyclone or hurricane'             },
    { value: 'TSUNAMI',    label: 'Tsunami',    icon: 'waves',                  color: 'text-cyan-400',   bg: 'border-cyan-400/40 bg-cyan-400/10',   description: 'Large ocean wave, coastal threat'               },
    { value: 'OTHER',      label: 'Other',      icon: 'warning',                color: 'text-on-surface-variant', bg: 'border-white/20 bg-white/5', description: 'Any other emergency situation'                   },
  ];

  severities = [
    { value: 'LOW',      label: 'Minor',    desc: 'Situation is manageable',          dot: 'bg-secondary',  ring: 'ring-secondary'  },
    { value: 'MEDIUM',   label: 'Moderate', desc: 'Needs attention soon',             dot: 'bg-amber-400',  ring: 'ring-amber-400'  },
    { value: 'HIGH',     label: 'Serious',  desc: 'Immediate response needed',        dot: 'bg-orange-400', ring: 'ring-orange-400' },
    { value: 'CRITICAL', label: 'Critical', desc: 'Life threatening — act NOW',       dot: 'bg-error',      ring: 'ring-error'      },
  ];

  constructor(
    private fb: FormBuilder,
    private svc: IncidentService,
    private router: Router
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.form = this.fb.group({
      disasterType: ['', Validators.required],
      severity:     ['', Validators.required],
      location:     ['', Validators.required],
      description:  ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(10)]],
    });
  }

  selectType(t: DisasterType): void {
    this.selectedType = t;
    this.form.patchValue({ disasterType: t.value });
  }

  selectSeverity(s: string): void {
    this.selectedSeverity = s;
    this.form.patchValue({ severity: s });
  }

  useGPS(): void {
    navigator.geolocation?.getCurrentPosition(p => {
      this.form.patchValue({
        location: `${p.coords.latitude.toFixed(4)}, ${p.coords.longitude.toFixed(4)}`
      });
    });
  }

  canProceed(): boolean {
    if (this.currentStep === 1) return !!(this.selectedType && this.selectedSeverity);
    if (this.currentStep === 2) {
      const c = this.form.get;
      return this.form.get('location')?.valid === true &&
        this.form.get('description')?.valid === true;
    }
    return true;
  }

  next(): void {
    if (this.canProceed() && this.currentStep < this.totalSteps) this.currentStep++;
  }

  back(): void {
    if (this.currentStep > 1) this.currentStep--;
  }

  onDragOver(e: DragEvent): void { e.preventDefault(); this.isDragOver = true; }
  onDragLeave(): void { this.isDragOver = false; }
  onDrop(e: DragEvent): void {
    e.preventDefault(); this.isDragOver = false;
    Array.from(e.dataTransfer?.files || []).forEach(f => this.addFile(f));
  }
  onFileSelect(e: Event): void {
    Array.from((e.target as HTMLInputElement).files || []).forEach(f => this.addFile(f));
  }

  addFile(file: File): void {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = ev => this.uploadedFiles.push({
      file, preview: ev.target?.result as string, uploading: false, done: false
    });
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }

  removeFile(i: number): void { this.uploadedFiles.splice(i, 1); }

  submit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) return;
    this.isLoading = true;

    this.svc.create(this.form.value).subscribe({
      next: report => {
        const pending = this.uploadedFiles.filter(f => !f.done);
        if (!pending.length) {
          this.isLoading = false;
          this.submitted = true;
          this.submittedId = report.id!;
          return;
        }
        let done = 0;
        pending.forEach(item => {
          item.uploading = true;
          this.svc.uploadEvidence(report.id!, item.file).subscribe({
            next: () => { item.uploading = false; item.done = true; done++; if (done === pending.length) { this.isLoading = false; this.submitted = true; this.submittedId = report.id!; } },
            error: () => { item.uploading = false; done++; if (done === pending.length) { this.isLoading = false; this.submitted = true; this.submittedId = report.id!; } }
          });
        });
      },
      error: err => { this.isLoading = false; this.errorMessage = err?.error?.message || 'Submission failed.'; }
    });
  }

  get progressPct(): number { return ((this.currentStep - 1) / (this.totalSteps - 1)) * 100; }

  get liveCredibility(): number {
    const v = this.form.value;
    let s = 0;
    if (v.description?.length > 100) s += 15;
    else if (v.description?.length > 50) s += 10;
    if (v.location?.trim()) s += 15;
    if (v.severity === 'HIGH' || v.severity === 'CRITICAL') s += 10;
    if (v.disasterType) s += 10;
    if (this.uploadedFiles.length > 0) s += 30;
    return Math.min(s, 100);
  }



  getSeverityLabel(): string {
    return this.severities.find(s => s.value === this.selectedSeverity)?.label || '';
  }

  appendTip(tip: string): void {
    const current = this.form.get('description')?.value || '';
    this.form.patchValue({ description: current + ' ' + tip });
  }

  getColorBg(colorClass: string): string {
    return colorClass.replace('text-', 'bg-');
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { Router, RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  FormBuilder,
  FormGroup,
  Validators,
  AbstractControl,
  ValidationErrors,
} from '@angular/forms';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/services/auth.service';

type ActiveTab = 'signin' | 'register';
type UserRole = 'VOLUNTEER' | 'ORGANISATION' | 'RESPONDER';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-register',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule, ReactiveFormsModule],
  templateUrl: './register.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./register.component.scss'],
})
export class RegisterComponent implements OnInit {
  activeTab: ActiveTab = 'signin';
  isLoading = false;
  showPassword = false;
  showConfirm = false;
  selectedRole: UserRole = 'VOLUNTEER';
  errorMessage = '';
  successMessage = '';

  signInForm!: FormGroup;
  registerForm!: FormGroup;

  roles = [
    {
      id: 'VOLUNTEER' as UserRole,
      label: 'Volunteer',
      desc: 'Help the community grow',
      icon: 'volunteer_activism',
      accent: 'primary',
      ringClass: 'ring-primary border-primary bg-primary/10',
      iconClass: 'text-primary bg-primary/20',
    },
    {
      id: 'ORGANISATION' as UserRole,
      label: 'Organisation',
      desc: 'Post opportunities & manage volunteers',
      icon: 'corporate_fare',
      accent: 'tertiary-dim',
      ringClass: 'ring-tertiary-dim border-tertiary-dim bg-tertiary-dim/10',
      iconClass: 'text-tertiary-dim bg-tertiary-dim/20',
    },
    {
      id: 'RESPONDER' as UserRole,
      label: 'Responder',
      desc: 'Emergency field operations',
      icon: 'emergency',
      accent: 'error',
      ringClass: 'ring-error border-error bg-error/10',
      iconClass: 'text-error bg-error/20',
    },
  ];

  constructor(
    private fb: FormBuilder,
    private authService: AuthService,
    private router: Router
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.signInForm = this.fb.group({
      email: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.email]],
      password: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(8)]],
    });

    this.registerForm = this.fb.group(
      {
        firstName: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(2)]],
        lastName: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(2)]],
        email: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.email]],
        password: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(8)]],
        confirmPassword: ['', Validators.required],
        role: [this.selectedRole, Validators.required],
      },
      { validators: this.passwordMatchValidator }
    );
  }

  passwordMatchValidator(group: AbstractControl): ValidationErrors | null {
    const pw = group.get('password')?.value;
    const cpw = group.get('confirmPassword')?.value;
    return pw === cpw ? null : { passwordMismatch: true };
  }

  get passwordStrength(): { score: number; label: string; color: string } {
    const pw = this.registerForm?.get('password')?.value || '';
    let score = 0;

    if (pw.length >= 8) score++;
    if (/[A-Z]/.test(pw)) score++;
    if (/[0-9]/.test(pw)) score++;
    if (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(pw)) score++;

    const levels = [
      { score: 0, label: '', color: '' },
      { score: 1, label: 'Weak', color: 'bg-error' },
      { score: 2, label: 'Fair', color: 'bg-amber-400' },
      { score: 3, label: 'Good', color: 'bg-secondary' },
      { score: 4, label: 'Strong', color: 'bg-tertiary-dim' },
    ];

    return levels[score] || levels[0];
  }

  isInvalid(form: FormGroup, field: string): boolean {
    const c = form.get(field);
    return !!(c && c.invalid && (c.dirty || c.touched));
  }

  getError(form: FormGroup, field: string): string {
    const c = form.get(field);
    if (!c || !c.errors) return '';

    if (c.errors['required']) return 'This field is required.';
    if (c.errors['email']) return 'Enter a valid email address.';
    if (c.errors['minlength']) {
      return `Minimum ${c.errors['minlength'].requiredLength} characters.`;
    }

    return '';
  }

  get passwordMismatch(): boolean {
    return !!(
      this.registerForm.errors?.['passwordMismatch'] &&
      this.registerForm.get('confirmPassword')?.touched
    );
  }

  selectRole(role: UserRole): void {
    this.selectedRole = role;
    this.registerForm.patchValue({ role });
  }

  getRoleActive(roleId: UserRole): boolean {
    return this.selectedRole === roleId;
  }

  switchTab(tab: ActiveTab): void {
    this.activeTab = tab;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
  }

private redirectByRole(role: string): void {
  const normalizedRole = (role || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const adminRoles = ['PLATFORM_ADMIN', 'ORG_ADMIN', 'AGENCY_ADMIN'];

  if (adminRoles.includes(normalizedRole)) {
    this.router.navigate(['/dashboard']);
  } else {
    this.router.navigate(['/home']);
  }
}

  onSignIn(): void {
    if (this.signInForm.invalid) {
      this.signInForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.isLoading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';

    const payload = {
      email: this.signInForm.value.email,
      password: this.signInForm.value.password,
    };

    this.authService.login(payload).subscribe({
      next: (res) => {
        console.log('LOGIN SUCCESS', res);
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.redirectByRole(res.role);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('LOGIN ERROR', err);
        this.isLoading = false;

        if (err?.error?.message) {
          this.errorMessage = err.error.message;
        } else if (err?.status === 401) {
          this.errorMessage = 'Invalid email or password.';
        } else if (err?.status === 0) {
          this.errorMessage = 'Cannot connect to the server.';
        } else {
          this.errorMessage = 'Login failed. Please try again.';
        }
      },
    });
  }

  onRegister(): void {
    if (this.registerForm.invalid) {
      this.registerForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.isLoading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';

    const formData = this.registerForm.value;

    const payload = {
      firstName: formData.firstName,
      lastName: formData.lastName,
      email: formData.email,
      password: formData.password,
      role: formData.role,
    };

    this.authService.register(payload).subscribe({
      next: (res) => {
        console.log('REGISTER SUCCESS', res);
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.successMessage = 'Account created successfully.';
        this.redirectByRole(res.role);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('REGISTER ERROR', err);
        this.isLoading = false;

        if (err?.error?.message) {
          this.errorMessage = err.error.message;
        } else if (err?.status === 400) {
          this.errorMessage = 'Invalid registration data.';
        } else if (err?.status === 0) {
          this.errorMessage = 'Cannot connect to the server.';
        } else {
          this.errorMessage = 'Registration failed. Please try again.';
        }
      },
    });
  }

  onGuestContinue(): void {
    console.log('Guest continue');
    this.router.navigate(['/landing']);
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\resource-form\resource-form.component.ts ===
import { Component, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  FormBuilder,
  Validators,
  FormsModule,
} from '@angular/forms';
import { Observable, map, switchMap, of } from 'rxjs';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { ResourceShelterService } from '../../core/services/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.service';
import {
  ResourceEntity,
  ResourcePayload,
  ResourceType,
} from '../../shared/models/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-resource-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    ReactiveFormsModule,
    FormsModule,
    RouterModule,
  ],
  templateUrl: './resource-form.component.html',
  styleUrl: './resource-form.component.scss',
})
export class ResourceFormComponent {
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);

  resources: ResourceEntity[] = [];
  isLoading = false;
  isSubmitting = false;
  isAllocating = false;
  successMessage = '';
  errorMessage = '';
  allocationMessage = '';
  editingId: number | null = null;

  readonly types: ResourceType[] = [
    'FOOD_SUPPLY',
    'MEDICAL_KIT',
    'VEHICLE',
    'EQUIPMENT',
    'GENERATOR',
    'BOAT',
  ];

  readonly form = this.fb.nonNullable.group({
    name: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(2)]],
    type: ['FOOD_SUPPLY' as ResourceType, Validators.required],
    quantity: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    locationUrl: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.pattern('https?://.+')]],
  });

  readonly allocationForm = this.fb.nonNullable.group({
    taskId: [1001, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    resourceId: [0, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    quantity: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
  });

  readonly dashboardStats = [
    {
      label: 'Total Resources',
      getValue: () => this.resources.length,
      color: 'text-primary',
    },
    {
      label: 'Resources Available',
      getValue: () =>
        this.resources.filter((item) => item.status === 'AVAILABLE').length,
      color: 'text-tertiary-dim',
    },
    {
      label: 'Active Alerts',
      getValue: () => 3,
      color: 'text-error',
    },
  ];

  constructor(private readonly resourceShelterService: ResourceShelterService) {
    this.loadResources();
  }

  loadResources(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.resourceShelterService.getAllResources().subscribe({
      next: (items) => {
        this.resources = items;
        if (items.length > 0 && this.allocationForm.getRawValue().resourceId === 0) {
          this.allocationForm.patchValue({ resourceId: items[0].resourceId });
        }
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Impossible de charger les resources.';
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
    });
  }

  submit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) {
      this.form.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.isSubmitting = true;

    const raw = this.form.getRawValue();
    this.resolveOwnerId().subscribe({
      next: (resolvedOwnerId) => {
        const payload: ResourcePayload = {
          ownerId: resolvedOwnerId,
          type: raw.type,
          name: raw.name.trim(),
          quantity: Number(raw.quantity),
          condition: 'GOOD',
          locationUrl: raw.locationUrl.trim(),
          status: 'AVAILABLE',
          allocatedToRequestId: null,
        };

        if (this.editingId === null) {
          this.resourceShelterService.createResource(payload).subscribe({
            next: () => {
              this.successMessage = 'Resource ajoutee avec succes.';
              this.afterMutation();
            },
            error: (err) => {
              const message = this.getErrorMessage(err);
              this.errorMessage = message;
              this.isSubmitting = false;
            },
          });
          return;
        }

        this.resourceShelterService.updateResource(this.editingId, payload).subscribe({
          next: () => {
            this.successMessage = 'Resource modifiee avec succes.';
            this.afterMutation();
          },
          error: (err) => {
            const message = this.getErrorMessage(err);
            this.errorMessage = message;
            this.isSubmitting = false;
          },
        });
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage = this.getErrorMessage(err);
        this.isSubmitting = false;
      },
    });
  }

  startEdit(item: ResourceEntity): void {
    this.editingId = item.resourceId;
    this.form.patchValue({
      name: item.name,
      type: item.type,
      quantity: item.quantity,
      locationUrl: item.locationUrl,
    });
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.errorMessage = '';
  }

  remove(item: ResourceEntity): void {
    const ok = window.confirm(`Supprimer la resource #${item.resourceId} ?`);
    if (!ok) {
      return;
    }

    this.resourceShelterService.deleteResource(item.resourceId).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.successMessage = 'Resource supprimee avec succes.';
        this.loadResources();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Erreur suppression resource.';
      },
    });
  }

  cancelEdit(): void {
    this.editingId = null;
    this.form.reset({
      name: '',
      type: 'FOOD_SUPPLY',
      quantity: 1,
      locationUrl: '',
    });
  }

  allocate(): void {
    if (this.allocationForm.invalid) {
      this.allocationForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    const raw = this.allocationForm.getRawValue();
    const resourceId = Number(raw.resourceId);
    const taskId = Number(raw.taskId);
    const qty = Number(raw.quantity);

    const selected = this.resources.find((item) => item.resourceId === resourceId);
    if (!selected) {
      this.allocationMessage = 'Resource introuvable.';
      return;
    }

    if (qty > selected.quantity) {
      this.allocationMessage = 'Quantite demandee superieure au stock.';
      return;
    }

    this.isAllocating = true;
    this.allocationMessage = '';

    const nextQuantity = selected.quantity - qty;
    const payload: ResourcePayload = {
      ownerId: this.ownerIdOf(selected),
      type: selected.type,
      name: selected.name,
      quantity: nextQuantity,
      condition: selected.condition,
      locationUrl: selected.locationUrl,
      status: nextQuantity === 0 ? 'DEPLOYED' : 'RESERVED',
      allocatedToRequestId: taskId,
    };

    this.resourceShelterService.updateResource(selected.resourceId, payload).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.allocationMessage = `Resource allouee a la mission ${taskId}.`;
        this.isAllocating = false;
        this.loadResources();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.allocationMessage = 'Echec allocation resource.';
        this.isAllocating = false;
      },
    });
  }

  ownerIdOf(item: ResourceEntity): number {
    if (item.owner && typeof item.owner.userId === 'number') {
      return item.owner.userId;
    }
    if (item.owner && typeof item.owner.id === 'number') {
      return item.owner.id;
    }
    return 1;
  }

  labelForType(type: ResourceType): string {
    const labels: Record<ResourceType, string> = {
      FOOD_SUPPLY: 'FOOD',
      MEDICAL_KIT: 'MEDICAL',
      VEHICLE: 'VEHICLE',
      EQUIPMENT: 'EQUIPMENT',
      GENERATOR: 'GENERATOR',
      BOAT: 'BOAT',
    };
    return labels[type];
  }

  private afterMutation(): void {
    this.isSubmitting = false;
    this.cancelEdit();
    this.loadResources();
  }

  private getErrorMessage(err: unknown): string {
    if (typeof err === 'object' && err !== null) {
      const withError = err as { error?: { message?: string } };
      if (withError.error && withError.error.message) {
        return withError.error.message;
      }
    }
    return 'Erreur lors de l operation resource.';
  }

  private resolveOwnerId(): Observable<number> {
    const userId = Number(localStorage.getItem('userId'));
    return of(userId > 0 ? userId : 1);
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\reviews\reviews.component.ts ===
import { Component } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { FrontOfficeLayoutComponent } from '../../layouts/front-office-layout/front-office-layout.component';
import { ReviewsTrustService } from '../../core/services/reviews-trust/reviews-trust.service';
import {
  ReviewEntity,
  ReviewTarget,
  TrustScoreEntity,
} from '../../shared/models/reviews/reviews-trust.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-reviews',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterModule, FrontOfficeLayoutComponent],
  templateUrl: './reviews.component.html',
  styleUrl: './reviews.component.scss',
})
export class ReviewsComponent {
  private readonly reactionsStorageKey = 'reviews-user-reactions';
  readonly stars = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];
  readonly reactionEmojis = ['👍', '❤️', '👏', '💡', '😂'];
  reviews: ReviewEntity[] = [];
  isLoading = false;
  message = '';
  errorMessage = '';
  selectedReactions: Record<number, string> = {};

  targetType: ReviewTarget = 'ORGANISATION';
  targetId = 1;

  trustScore: TrustScoreEntity | null = null;
  trustErrorMessage = '';

  constructor(private readonly reviewsTrustService: ReviewsTrustService) {
    this.loadStoredReactions();
    this.loadAllReviews();
  }

  get averageRating(): number {
    if (this.reviews.length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    const total = this.reviews.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.rating, 0);
    return total / this.reviews.length;
  }

  get publishedCount(): number {
    return this.reviews.filter((item) => item.status === 'PUBLISHED').length;
  }

  loadAllReviews(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.message = '';
    this.trustScore = null;

    this.reviewsTrustService.getAllReviews().subscribe({
      next: (items) => {
        this.reviews = items;
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Unable to load reviews.';
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
    });
  }

  loadByTarget(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.message = '';

    this.reviewsTrustService
      .getReviewsByTarget(this.targetId, this.targetType)
      .subscribe({
        next: (items) => {
          this.reviews = items;
          this.isLoading = false;
          this.message = 'Reviews filtered by target.';
          this.loadTrustScore();
        },
        error: () => {
          this.errorMessage = 'Unable to filter reviews by target.';
          this.isLoading = false;
          this.trustScore = null;
        },
      });
  }

  reactWithEmoji(review: ReviewEntity, emoji: string): void {
    const reviewId = review.reviewId;
    const previous = this.selectedReactions[reviewId];

    if (previous === emoji) {
      return;
    }

    this.selectedReactions[reviewId] = emoji;
    this.saveStoredReactions();
  }

  toggleLike(review: ReviewEntity): void {
    const reviewId = review.reviewId;
    if (this.selectedReactions[reviewId]) {
      delete this.selectedReactions[reviewId];
      this.saveStoredReactions();
      return;
    }

    this.selectedReactions[reviewId] = '👍';
    this.saveStoredReactions();
  }

  displayedLikes(review: ReviewEntity): number {
    const hasReaction = !!this.selectedReactions[review.reviewId];
    return review.helpfulVotes + (hasReaction ? 1 : 0);
  }

  selectedReactionEmoji(reviewId: number): string {
    const selected = this.selectedReactions[reviewId];
    if (selected) {
      return selected;
    }
    return '👍';
  }

  isReactionSelected(reviewId: number, emoji: string): boolean {
    return this.selectedReactions[reviewId] === emoji;
  }

  isReviewStarActive(review: ReviewEntity, star: number): boolean {
    return star <= review.rating;
  }

  targetTypeLabel(targetType: ReviewTarget): 'RESOURCE' | 'SHELTER' {
    if (targetType === 'ORGANISATION') {
      return 'RESOURCE';
    }
    return 'SHELTER';
  }

  private loadTrustScore(): void {
    this.trustErrorMessage = '';
    this.reviewsTrustService
      .getTrustScoreByEntity(this.targetId, this.targetType)
      .subscribe({
        next: (score) => {
          this.trustScore = score;
        },
        error: () => {
          this.trustScore = null;
          this.trustErrorMessage =
            'No trust score is currently available for this target.';
        },
      });
  }

  private loadStoredReactions(): void {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(this.reactionsStorageKey);
      if (!raw) {
        this.selectedReactions = {};
        return;
      }

      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, string>;
      const normalized: Record<number, string> = {};
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(parsed)) {
        const reviewId = Number(key);
        if (!Number.isNaN(reviewId) && this.reactionEmojis.includes(value)) {
          normalized[reviewId] = value;
        }
      }
      this.selectedReactions = normalized;
    } catch {
      this.selectedReactions = {};
    }
  }

  private saveStoredReactions(): void {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(
        this.reactionsStorageKey,
        JSON.stringify(this.selectedReactions),
      );
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage failures and keep reactions in memory.
    }
  }
}

                <button (click)="showAddCheckpoint = !showAddCheckpoint"
                        class="text-primary text-xs font-bold hover:text-primary/70 transition-colors">
                  + Add
                </button>
              </div>

              <!-- Add checkpoint form with map picker -->
              <div *ngIf="showAddCheckpoint" class="mb-4 p-4 bg-white/5 rounded-xl space-y-3">

                        class="w-full py-2 bg-primary text-on-primary rounded-lg text-sm font-bold">
                  Add Checkpoint
                </button>
              </div>

              <div class="space-y-2">
                <div *ngFor="let cp of checkpoints"
                     class="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-white/5 rounded-xl text-sm">
                  <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-sm">
                    {{ cp.type === 'MEDICAL_STOP' ? 'local_hospital' : cp.type === 'SUPPLY_POINT' ? 'inventory' : 'place' }}
                  </span>
                  <div class="flex-1">
                    <p class="font-medium">{{ cp.label }}</p>
                    <p class="text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant font-mono">{{ cp.location }}</p>

                  <span class="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/5 text-on-surface-variant">{{ cp.type }}</span>
                </div>
                <p *ngIf="checkpoints.length === 0" class="text-xs text-on-surface-variant text-center py-4">
                  No checkpoints yet
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Map showing route path + checkpoints -->
          <div class="lg:col-span-3 glass-1 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-white/5" style="height: 520px;">
            <app-map-view [geometries]="routeGeometries"></app-map-view>
          </div>
        </div>
      </ng-container>
    </div>
  `
})
export class RouteDetailComponent implements OnInit {
  route?: EvacuationRoute;
  checkpoints: RouteCheckpoint[] = [];
  isNew = false;
  showAddCheckpoint = false;

  formData: any = { alertId: null, toShelterId: null, estimatedDurationMin: null, capacity: null };
  newCheckpoint: any = { label: '', type: 'WAYPOINT', location: '', isActive: true };

  get routeGeometries() {
    const geoms: any[] = [];
    if (this.route?.path) geoms.push({ type: 'LineString', wkt: this.route.path, color: '#6C5CE7' });
    this.checkpoints.forEach(cp => {
      if (cp.location) geoms.push({ type: 'Point', wkt: cp.location, color: '#7DEFC0' });
    });
    return geoms;
  }

  constructor(
    private route_: ActivatedRoute,
    private router: Router,
    private evacService: EvacuationService
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const idParam = this.route_.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    if (idParam === 'new') { this.isNew = true; return; }
    const id = Number(idParam);
    if (!isNaN(id)) {
      this.evacService.getRouteById(id).subscribe(data => {
        this.route = data;
        this.evacService.getCheckpointsByRoute(id).subscribe(cps => this.checkpoints = cps);
      });
    }
  }

  onCheckpointLocationPicked(wkt: string): void { this.newCheckpoint.location = wkt; }

  createRoute(): void {
    this.evacService.createRoute(this.formData).subscribe(() => this.router.navigate(['/evacuation']));
  }

  addCheckpoint(): void {
    if (!this.route || !this.newCheckpoint.location) return;
    const payload = { ...this.newCheckpoint, routeId: this.route.id };
    this.evacService.createCheckpoint(payload).subscribe(cp => {
      this.checkpoints.push(cp);
      this.newCheckpoint = { label: '', type: 'WAYPOINT', location: '', isActive: true };
      this.showAddCheckpoint = false;
    });
  }

  activate(): void { if (this.route) this.evacService.activateRoute(this.route.id).subscribe(r => this.route = r); }
  close(): void    { if (this.route) this.evacService.closeRoute(this.route.id).subscribe(r => this.route = r); }

  deleteRoute(): void {
    if (!this.route) return;
    if (confirm('Delete this evacuation route?')) {
      this.evacService.deleteRoute(this.route.id).subscribe(() => this.router.navigate(['/evacuation']));
    }
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { FormBuilder, FormGroup, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';

@Component({

                <option value="WEATHER">Weather</option>
                <option value="AIR_QUALITY">Air Quality</option>
                <option value="FIRE_SMOKE">Fire / Smoke</option>
              </select>

            <div class="space-y-1">
              <label class="text-xs font-bold text-on-surface-variant uppercase tracking-wide">Manufacturer</label>
              <input formControlName="manufacturer"
                     class="w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-on-surface outline-none focus:border-primary/50 transition-colors" />
            </div>

            <div class="space-y-1">
              <label class="text-xs font-bold text-on-surface-variant uppercase tracking-wide">Owner ID</label>
              <input type="number" formControlName="ownerId"
                     class="w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-on-surface outline-none focus:border-primary/50 transition-colors" />
            </div>

            <div *ngIf="sensorForm.get('location')?.value" class="p-3 bg-primary/10 border border-primary/20 rounded-xl text-xs">
              <span class="text-on-surface-variant">Location: </span>
              <span class="font-mono text-primary">{{ sensorForm.get('location')?.value }}</span>
            </div>

            <div class="flex gap-3 pt-2">
              <button type="submit" [disabled]="sensorForm.invalid"
                      class="flex-1 bg-primary text-on-primary py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm hover:shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(108,92,231,0.4)] transition-all disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
                Create Sensor
              </button>
              <a [routerLink]="basePath"
                 class="px-5 py-3 rounded-xl border border-white/20 text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-white/5 transition-all font-bold text-sm">
                Cancel
              </a>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="glass-1 p-6 rounded-2xl border border-white/5">
            <h2 class="font-bold text-on-surface-variant text-xs uppercase tracking-widest mb-4">Pin Location *</h2>

            </div>

            <h2 class="font-bold text-on-surface-variant text-xs uppercase tracking-widest mb-4">Update Location</h2>
            <app-map-picker
              [initialWkt]="sensorForm.get('location')?.value"
              (locationPicked)="onLocationPicked($event)">
            </app-map-picker>
          </div>
        </form>
      </ng-container>
    </div>
  `
})
export class SensorDetailComponent implements OnInit {
  sensor?: Sensor;
  isNew    = false;
  editMode = false;
  basePath = '/sensors';
  sensorForm: FormGroup;

  constructor(
    private route: ActivatedRoute,
    private router: Router,
    private fb: FormBuilder,
    private sensorService: SensorService,
    private authService: AuthService
  ) {
    this.sensorForm = this.fb.group({
      name:         ['', Validators.required],
      type:         ['SEISMIC', Validators.required],
      location:     ['', Validators.required],
      manufacturer: [''],
      ownerId:      [null]
    });
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const role = (this.authService.getRole() || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    this.basePath = ADMIN_ROLES.includes(role) ? '/admin/sensors' : '/sensors';

    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    if (idParam === 'new') { this.isNew = true; return; }
    const id = Number(idParam);
    if (!isNaN(id)) {
      this.sensorService.getSensorById(id).subscribe(data => {
        this.sensor = data;
        this.sensorForm.patchValue(data);
      });
    }
  }

  onLocationPicked(wkt: string): void { this.sensorForm.patchValue({ location: wkt }); }

  createSensor(): void {
    if (this.sensorForm.invalid) return;
    this.sensorService.createSensor(this.sensorForm.value)
      .subscribe(() => this.router.navigate([this.basePath]));
  }

  enableEdit(): void  { this.editMode = true; }
  cancelEdit(): void  { this.editMode = false; if (this.sensor) this.sensorForm.patchValue(this.sensor); }

  updateSensor(): void {
    if (!this.sensor || this.sensorForm.invalid) return;
    this.sensorService.updateSensor(this.sensor.id, this.sensorForm.value).subscribe(updated => {
      this.sensor   = updated;
      this.editMode = false;
    });
  }

  deleteSensor(): void {
    if (!this.sensor) return;
    if (confirm('Delete this sensor permanently?')) {
      this.sensorService.deleteSensor(this.sensor.id)
        .subscribe(() => this.router.navigate([this.basePath]));
    }
  }

  ping(): void {
    if (this.sensor) {
      this.sensorService.updateLastPing(this.sensor.id).subscribe(u => this.sensor = u);
    }
  }
}

export class SensorListComponent implements OnInit {
  sensors: Sensor[]  = [];
  filtered: Sensor[] = [];
  searchTerm   = '';
  statusFilter = '';
  typeFilter   = '';
  isAdmin      = false;
  basePath     = '/sensors';

  constructor(
    private sensorService: SensorService,
    private authService: AuthService
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const role = (this.authService.getRole() || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    this.isAdmin  = ADMIN_ROLES.includes(role);
    this.basePath = this.isAdmin ? '/admin/sensors' : '/sensors';

    this.sensorService.getAllSensors().subscribe(data => {
      this.sensors  = data;
      this.filtered = data;
    });
  }

  applyFilters(): void {
    this.filtered = this.sensors.filter(s => {
      const matchSearch = !this.searchTerm ||
        s.name.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
        (s.manufacturer || '').toLowerCase().includes(this.searchTerm.toLowerCase());
      const matchStatus = !this.statusFilter || s.status === this.statusFilter;
      const matchType   = !this.typeFilter   || s.type   === this.typeFilter;
      return matchSearch && matchStatus && matchType;
    });
  }

  typeIcon(type: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      SEISMIC: 'earthquake', FLOOD_GAUGE: 'water', WEATHER: 'thunderstorm',
      AIR_QUALITY: 'air', FIRE_SMOKE: 'local_fire_department'
    };
    return map[type] ?? 'sensors';
  }
  typeIconBg(type: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      SEISMIC: 'bg-orange-500/20', FLOOD_GAUGE: 'bg-blue-500/20', WEATHER: 'bg-sky-500/20',
      AIR_QUALITY: 'bg-green-500/20', FIRE_SMOKE: 'bg-red-500/20'
    };
    return map[type] ?? 'bg-white/10';
  }
  typeIconColor(type: string): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      SEISMIC: 'text-orange-400', FLOOD_GAUGE: 'text-blue-400', WEATHER: 'text-sky-400',
      AIR_QUALITY: 'text-green-400', FIRE_SMOKE: 'text-red-400'
    };
    return map[type] ?? 'text-white';
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\shelter-details\shelter-details.component.ts ===
import { Component } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { ActivatedRoute, RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { ResourceShelterService } from '../../core/services/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.service';
import { ShelterEntity } from '../../shared/models/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-shelter-details',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule],
  templateUrl: './shelter-details.component.html',
})
export class ShelterDetailsComponent {
  shelter: ShelterEntity | null = null;
  isLoading = true;

  constructor(
    private readonly route: ActivatedRoute,
    private readonly resourceShelterService: ResourceShelterService,
  ) {
    this.loadDetails();
  }

  loadDetails(): void {
    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    const shelterId = Number(idParam);

    this.resourceShelterService.getAllShelters().subscribe({
      next: (items) => {
        const found = items.find((item) => item.shelterId === shelterId);
        if (found) {
          this.shelter = found;
        }
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
    });
  }

  remaining(): number {
    if (!this.shelter) {
      return 0;
    }

    const value = this.shelter.capacity - this.shelter.currentOccupancy;
    if (value < 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    return value;
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\shelter-form\shelter-form.component.ts ===
import { Component, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { ReactiveFormsModule, FormBuilder, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { Observable, map, of, switchMap } from 'rxjs';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { ResourceShelterService } from '../../core/services/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.service';
import {
  ShelterEntity,
  ShelterOccupantEntity,
  ShelterPayload,
  ShelterStatus,
} from '../../shared/models/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-shelter-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    ReactiveFormsModule,
    RouterModule,
  ],
  templateUrl: './shelter-form.component.html',
  styleUrl: './shelter-form.component.scss',
})
export class ShelterFormComponent {
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);

  shelters: ShelterEntity[] = [];
  editingId: number | null = null;
  isLoading = false;
  isSubmitting = false;
  successMessage = '';
  errorMessage = '';

  readonly statuses: ShelterStatus[] = ['OPEN', 'FULL', 'CLOSED', 'PREPARING'];

  readonly shelterTypes = ['LOCAL', 'MEDICAL'];

  readonly form = this.fb.nonNullable.group({
    name: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(2)]],
    locationUrl: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.pattern('https?://.+')]],
    address: [''],
    capacity: [10, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    shelterType: ['LOCAL', Validators.required],
  });

  constructor(private readonly resourceShelterService: ResourceShelterService) {
    this.loadShelters();
  }

  loadShelters(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.resourceShelterService.getAllShelters().subscribe({
      next: (items) => {
        this.shelters = items;
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Impossible de charger les shelters.';
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
    });
  }

  submit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) {
      this.form.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.isSubmitting = true;

    const raw = this.form.getRawValue();
    this.resolveManagerId().subscribe({
      next: (resolvedManagerId) => {
        const payload: ShelterPayload = {
          name: raw.name.trim(),
          locationUrl: raw.locationUrl.trim(),
          address: raw.address.trim() || 'N/A',
          capacity: Number(raw.capacity),
          managerId: resolvedManagerId,
          amenities: [`TYPE:${raw.shelterType}`],
          status: 'OPEN',
        };

        if (this.editingId === null) {
          this.resourceShelterService.createShelter(payload).subscribe({
            next: () => {
              this.successMessage = 'Shelter ajoute avec succes.';
              this.afterMutation();
            },
            error: (err) => {
              this.errorMessage = this.getErrorMessage(err);
              this.isSubmitting = false;
            },
          });
          return;
        }

        this.resourceShelterService.updateShelter(this.editingId, payload).subscribe({
          next: () => {
            this.successMessage = 'Shelter modifie avec succes.';
            this.afterMutation();
          },
          error: (err) => {
            this.errorMessage = this.getErrorMessage(err);
            this.isSubmitting = false;
          },
        });
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage = this.getErrorMessage(err);
        this.isSubmitting = false;
      },
    });
  }

  startEdit(item: ShelterEntity): void {
    this.editingId = item.shelterId;
    this.form.patchValue({
      name: item.name,
      locationUrl: item.locationUrl,
      address: item.address ?? '',
      capacity: item.capacity,
      shelterType: this.resolveTypeForForm(item),
    });
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.errorMessage = '';
  }

  cancelEdit(): void {
    this.editingId = null;
    this.form.reset({
      name: '',
      locationUrl: '',
      address: '',
      capacity: 10,
      shelterType: 'LOCAL',
    });
  }

  remove(item: ShelterEntity): void {
    const ok = window.confirm(`Supprimer le shelter #${item.shelterId} ?`);
    if (!ok) {
      return;
    }

    this.resourceShelterService.deleteShelter(item.shelterId).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.successMessage = 'Shelter supprime avec succes.';
        this.loadShelters();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage = this.getErrorMessage(err);
      },
    });
  }

  checkIn(item: ShelterEntity): void {
    const current = this.displayOccupancy(item);
    if (current >= item.capacity) {
      return;
    }

    this.resolveManagerId()
      .pipe(
        switchMap((resolvedUserId) =>
          this.resourceShelterService.checkInShelter({
            shelterId: item.shelterId,
            userId: resolvedUserId,
          }),
        ),
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage = 'Occupancy updated successfully.';
          this.errorMessage = '';
          this.loadShelters();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.errorMessage = this.getErrorMessage(err);
        },
      });
  }

  checkOut(item: ShelterEntity): void {
    const current = this.displayOccupancy(item);
    if (current <= 0) {
      return;
    }

    this.resourceShelterService
      .getShelterOccupantsByShelter(item.shelterId)
      .pipe(
        map((records) => this.latestActiveOccupant(records)),
        switchMap((activeRecord) => {
          if (!activeRecord) {
            throw new Error('No active occupant record');
          }
          return this.resourceShelterService.checkOutShelter(activeRecord.recordId);
        }),
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage = 'Occupancy updated successfully.';
          this.errorMessage = '';
          this.loadShelters();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.errorMessage = this.getErrorMessage(err);
        },
      });
  }

  displayOccupancy(item: ShelterEntity): number {
    const value = item.currentOccupancy;
    if (value < 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    if (value > item.capacity) {
      return item.capacity;
    }
    return value;
  }

  remainingCapacity(item: ShelterEntity): number {
    return item.capacity - this.displayOccupancy(item);
  }

  availabilityStatus(item: ShelterEntity): string {
    if (this.remainingCapacity(item) <= 0) {
      return 'FULL';
    }
    return 'AVAILABLE';
  }

  shelterType(item: ShelterEntity): string {
    const typeAmenity = item.amenities?.find((entry) =>
      (entry.amenity ?? '').startsWith('TYPE:'),
    );
    if (!typeAmenity) {
      return 'N/A';
    }
    return (typeAmenity.amenity ?? '').replace('TYPE:', '') || 'N/A';
  }

  private resolveTypeForForm(item: ShelterEntity): string {
    const type = this.shelterType(item);
    if (this.shelterTypes.includes(type)) {
      return type;
    }
    return 'LOCAL';
  }

  private afterMutation(): void {
    this.isSubmitting = false;
    this.cancelEdit();
    this.loadShelters();
  }

  private latestActiveOccupant(
    records: ShelterOccupantEntity[],
  ): ShelterOccupantEntity | null {
    const active = records.filter((record) => !record.checkOutAt);
    if (active.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    active.sort((a, b) => {
      const aTime = new Date(a.checkInAt).getTime();
      const bTime = new Date(b.checkInAt).getTime();
      return bTime - aTime;
    });

    return active[0];
  }

  private getErrorMessage(err: unknown): string {
    if (typeof err === 'object' && err !== null) {
      const withError = err as { error?: { message?: string } };
      if (withError.error && withError.error.message) {
        return withError.error.message;
      }
    }
    return 'Erreur lors de la mise a jour du shelter.';
  }

  private resolveManagerId(): Observable<number> {
    const userId = Number(localStorage.getItem('userId'));
    return of(userId > 0 ? userId : 1);
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\shelter-recommender\shelter-recommender.component.ts ===
import { Component } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { FrontOfficeLayoutComponent } from '../../layouts/front-office-layout/front-office-layout.component';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { environment } from '../../../environments/environment';

interface RecommendationRequest {
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  maxDistanceKm: number;
  needsMedical: boolean;
  needsAccessible: boolean;
  hasPets: boolean;
  peopleCount: number;
}

interface RecommendationItem {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  type: 'LOCAL' | 'MEDICAL';
  status: 'AVAILABLE' | 'FULL' | 'CLOSED';
  capacity: number;
  occupancy: number;
  remaining: number;
  distanceKm: number;
  score: number;
  reasons: string[];
}

interface RecommendationResponse {
  recommendations: RecommendationItem[];
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-shelter-recommender',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, FrontOfficeLayoutComponent],
  templateUrl: './shelter-recommender.component.html',
  styleUrl: './shelter-recommender.component.scss',
})
export class ShelterRecommenderComponent {
  request: RecommendationRequest = {
    lat: 36.8065,
    lng: 10.1815,
    maxDistanceKm: 40,
    needsMedical: false,
    needsAccessible: false,
    hasPets: false,
    peopleCount: 1,
  };

  isLoading = false;
  hasSearched = false;
  errorMessage = '';
  recommendations: RecommendationItem[] = [];

  constructor(private readonly http: HttpClient) {}

  findRecommendations(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.hasSearched = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.recommendations = [];

    const payload: RecommendationRequest = {
      lat: Number(this.request.lat),
      lng: Number(this.request.lng),
      maxDistanceKm: Number(this.request.maxDistanceKm),
      needsMedical: !!this.request.needsMedical,
      needsAccessible: !!this.request.needsAccessible,
      hasPets: !!this.request.hasPets,
      peopleCount: Math.max(1, Number(this.request.peopleCount) || 1),
    };

    this.http
      .post<RecommendationResponse>(
        `${environment.apiUrl}/shelters/recommend`,
        payload,
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: (response) => {
          this.recommendations = response.recommendations ?? [];
          this.isLoading = false;
        },
        error: () => {
          this.errorMessage =
            'Unable to load recommendations. Please check that backend is running on port 8090.';
          this.isLoading = false;
        },
      });
  }

  isAvailable(item: RecommendationItem): boolean {
    return item.status === 'AVAILABLE' && item.remaining > 0;
  }
}

                Click to focus on map
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

})
export class SpatialDashboardComponent implements OnInit, AfterViewInit, OnDestroy {
  @ViewChild('spatialMap') mapRef!: ElementRef;

  mode: QueryMode | null = null;
  polygonInput = '';
  nearLat = 36.8065;
  nearLng = 10.1815;
  nearLimit = 5;
  sensorResults: SpatialSensor[] = [];
  running = false;
  ran = false;

  private map!: L.Map;
  private resultLayers: L.Layer[] = [];
  private originMarker: L.Marker | null = null;

  constructor(
    private spatialService: SpatialService,
    private cdr: ChangeDetectorRef
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {}

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    setTimeout(() => this.initMap(), 50);
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.map) this.map.remove();
  }

  setMode(m: QueryMode): void {
    this.mode = m;
    this.sensorResults = [];
    this.ran = false;
    this.clearResultLayers();
    if (this.map) {
      this.map.getContainer().style.cursor = m === 'nearest' ? 'crosshair' : '';
      setTimeout(() => this.map.invalidateSize(), 50);
    }
  }

  useMapBounds(): void {
    if (!this.map) return;
    const b = this.map.getBounds();
    const sw = b.getSouthWest(), ne = b.getNorthEast();
    this.polygonInput = `POLYGON((${sw.lng.toFixed(6)} ${sw.lat.toFixed(6)}, ${ne.lng.toFixed(6)} ${sw.lat.toFixed(6)}, ${ne.lng.toFixed(6)} ${ne.lat.toFixed(6)}, ${sw.lng.toFixed(6)} ${ne.lat.toFixed(6)}, ${sw.lng.toFixed(6)} ${sw.lat.toFixed(6)}))`;
  }

  runInsideZone(): void {
    if (!this.polygonInput.trim()) return;
    this.running = true;
    this.clearResultLayers();
    this.spatialService.getSensorsInsideZone(this.polygonInput).subscribe({
      next: s => {
        this.sensorResults = s;
        this.running = false;
        this.ran = true;
        this.renderPolygon(this.polygonInput);
        this.renderSensorMarkers(s, '#6c5ce7');
      },
      error: () => { this.running = false; this.ran = true; }
    });
  }

  runNearest(): void {
    const point = `POINT(${this.nearLng} ${this.nearLat})`;
    this.running = true;
    this.clearResultLayers();
    this.spatialService.getNearestSensors(point, this.nearLimit).subscribe({
      next: s => {
        this.sensorResults = s;
        this.running = false;
        this.ran = true;
        this.renderOriginMarker(this.nearLat, this.nearLng);
        this.renderSensorMarkers(s, '#00b894');
        this.renderDistanceLines(s, this.nearLat, this.nearLng);
      },
      error: () => { this.running = false; this.ran = true; }
    });
  }

  focusSensor(s: SpatialSensor): void {
    if (!this.map || !s.location) return;
    try {
      const g = wkt.parse(s.location);
      if (g?.type === 'Point') this.map.setView([g.coordinates[1], g.coordinates[0]], 15, { animate: true });
    } catch {}
  }

  private initMap(): void {
    if (!this.mapRef?.nativeElement || this.map) return;

    this.map = L.map(this.mapRef.nativeElement, {
      zoomControl: false,
      scrollWheelZoom: true,
      preferCanvas: true
    }).setView([36.8065, 10.1815], 9);

    L.control.zoom({ position: 'topright' }).addTo(this.map);

    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/dark_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png', {
      attribution: '© OSM © CartoDB',
      subdomains: 'abcd',
      maxZoom: 19
    }).addTo(this.map);

    this.map.on('click', (e: L.LeafletMouseEvent) => {
      if (this.mode !== 'nearest') return;
      this.nearLat = parseFloat(e.latlng.lat.toFixed(6));
      this.nearLng = parseFloat(e.latlng.lng.toFixed(6));
      this.cdr.detectChanges();

      if (this.originMarker) this.map.removeLayer(this.originMarker);
      const icon = L.divIcon({
        html: `<div style="width:18px;height:18px;background:#f59e0b;border:3px solid white;border-radius:50%;box-shadow:0 0 14px rgba(245,158,11,0.9)"></div>`,
        iconSize: [18, 18], iconAnchor: [9, 9], className: ''
      });
      this.originMarker = L.marker([this.nearLat, this.nearLng], { icon })
        .bindPopup(`<b>Origin</b><br>${this.nearLat.toFixed(5)}, ${this.nearLng.toFixed(5)}`)
        .addTo(this.map);
    });

    setTimeout(() => this.map.invalidateSize(), 200);
  }

  private clearResultLayers(): void {
    this.resultLayers.forEach(l => this.map?.removeLayer(l));
    this.resultLayers = [];
    if (this.originMarker) {
      this.map?.removeLayer(this.originMarker);
      this.originMarker = null;
    }
  }

  private renderSensorMarkers(sensors: SpatialSensor[], color: string): void {
    sensors.forEach(s => {
      if (!s.location) return;
      try {
        const g = wkt.parse(s.location);
        if (g?.type === 'Point') {
          const m = L.circleMarker([g.coordinates[1], g.coordinates[0]], {
            radius: 10,
            color,
            fillColor: color,
            fillOpacity: 0.85,
            weight: 2
          }).bindPopup(`<b>${s.name}</b><br><i>${s.type.replace('_', ' ')}</i><br>Status: ${s.status}`);
          m.addTo(this.map);
          this.resultLayers.push(m);
        }
      } catch {}
    });

    if (this.resultLayers.filter(l => l instanceof L.CircleMarker).length > 0) {
      const markers = this.resultLayers.filter(l => l instanceof L.CircleMarker);
      const grp = L.featureGroup(markers);
      const b = grp.getBounds();
      if (b.isValid()) this.map.fitBounds(b, { padding: [60, 60], maxZoom: 13 });
    }
  }

  private renderOriginMarker(lat: number, lng: number): void {
    if (this.originMarker) this.map.removeLayer(this.originMarker);
    const icon = L.divIcon({
      html: `<div style="width:18px;height:18px;background:#f59e0b;border:3px solid white;border-radius:50%;box-shadow:0 0 14px rgba(245,158,11,0.9)"></div>`,
      iconSize: [18, 18], iconAnchor: [9, 9], className: ''
    });
    this.originMarker = L.marker([lat, lng], { icon })
      .bindPopup(`<b>Origin</b><br>${lat.toFixed(5)}, ${lng.toFixed(5)}`)
      .addTo(this.map);
  }

  private renderDistanceLines(sensors: SpatialSensor[], lat: number, lng: number): void {
    sensors.forEach(s => {
      if (!s.location) return;
      try {
        const g = wkt.parse(s.location);
        if (g?.type === 'Point') {
          const l = L.polyline(
            [[lat, lng], [g.coordinates[1], g.coordinates[0]]],
            { color: '#00b894', weight: 1.5, dashArray: '6,5', opacity: 0.5 }
          ).addTo(this.map);
          this.resultLayers.push(l);
        }
      } catch {}
    });
  }

  private renderPolygon(polygonWkt: string): void {
    try {
      const g = wkt.parse(polygonWkt);
      if (g?.type === 'Polygon') {
        const latLngs = g.coordinates[0].map((c: number[]) => [c[1], c[0]]) as [number, number][];
        const p = L.polygon(latLngs, {
          color: '#6c5ce7',
          fillColor: '#6c5ce7',
          fillOpacity: 0.10,
          weight: 2,
          dashArray: '8,5'
        }).addTo(this.map);
        this.resultLayers.push(p);
        this.map.fitBounds(p.getBounds(), { padding: [30, 30] });
      }
    } catch {}
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\pages\users-management\users-management.component.ts ===
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { UserService } from '../../core/services/user.service';
import { User, UserRequest } from '../../modules/identity/models/user.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-users-management',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './users-management.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./users-management.component.scss']
})
export class UsersManagementComponent implements OnInit {
  users: User[] = [];
  filteredUsers: User[] = [];
  loading = false;
  errorMessage = '';
  successMessage = '';
  searchTerm = '';

  showModal = false;
  isEditMode = false;
  selectedUserId: number | null = null;
  saving = false;

  formData: UserRequest = {
    email: '',
    password: '',
    role: 'CITIZEN',
    firstName: '',
    lastName: '',
    phone: '',
    location: '',
    profilePhoto: '',
    emailVerified: false,
    phoneVerified: false,
    mfaEnabled: false,
    status: 'ACTIVE'
  };

  constructor(private userService: UserService) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadUsers();
  }

  loadUsers(): void {
    this.loading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';

    this.userService.getAllUsers().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.users = data;
        this.applyFilter();
        this.loading = false;
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Load users error:', err);
        this.errorMessage = `Failed to load users (${err.status || 'unknown error'})`;
        this.loading = false;
      }
    });
  }

  applyFilter(): void {
    const term = this.searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!term) {
      this.filteredUsers = [...this.users];
      return;
    }

    this.filteredUsers = this.users.filter((user) =>
      user.firstName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      user.lastName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      user.email.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      user.role.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      (user.status || '').toLowerCase().includes(term)
    );
  }

  openAddModal(): void {
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.isEditMode = false;
    this.selectedUserId = null;

    this.formData = {
      email: '',
      password: '',
      role: 'CITIZEN',
      firstName: '',
      lastName: '',
      phone: '',
      location: '',
      profilePhoto: '',
      emailVerified: false,
      phoneVerified: false,
      mfaEnabled: false,
      status: 'ACTIVE'
    };

    this.showModal = true;
  }

  openEditModal(user: User): void {
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.isEditMode = true;
    this.selectedUserId = user.userId;

    this.formData = {
      email: user.email,
      password: '',
      role: user.role,
      firstName: user.firstName,
      lastName: user.lastName,
      phone: user.phone || '',
      location: user.location || '',
      profilePhoto: user.profilePhoto || '',
      emailVerified: user.emailVerified ?? false,
      phoneVerified: user.phoneVerified ?? false,
      mfaEnabled: user.mfaEnabled ?? false,
      status: user.status || 'ACTIVE'
    };

    this.showModal = true;
  }

  closeModal(): void {
    this.showModal = false;
    this.saving = false;
  }

  submitForm(): void {
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';

    if (!this.formData.firstName || !this.formData.lastName || !this.formData.email || !this.formData.role) {
      this.errorMessage = 'Please fill in the required fields.';
      return;
    }

    if (!this.isEditMode && !this.formData.password) {
      this.errorMessage = 'Password is required when creating a user.';
      return;
    }

    this.saving = true;

    if (this.isEditMode && this.selectedUserId !== null) {
      this.userService.updateUser(this.selectedUserId, this.formData).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage = 'User updated successfully.';
          this.saving = false;
          this.closeModal();
          this.loadUsers();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          console.error('Update user error:', err);
          this.errorMessage = err?.error?.message || 'Failed to update user.';
          this.saving = false;
        }
      });
    } else {
      this.userService.createUser(this.formData).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage = 'User created successfully.';
          this.saving = false;
          this.closeModal();
          this.loadUsers();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          console.error('Create user error:', err);
          this.errorMessage = err?.error?.message || 'Failed to create user.';
          this.saving = false;
        }
      });
    }
  }

  deleteUser(id: number): void {
    if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this user?')) {
      return;
    }

    this.userService.deleteUser(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.loadUsers();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Delete user error:', err);
        this.errorMessage = err?.error?.message || `Failed to delete user (${err.status || 'unknown error'})`;
      }
    });
  }

  getRoleClass(role?: string): string {
    switch ((role || '').toUpperCase()) {
      case 'PLATFORM_ADMIN':
        return 'role-admin';
      case 'ORG_ADMIN':
      case 'AGENCY_ADMIN':
        return 'role-manager';
      case 'RESPONDER':
        return 'role-responder';
      case 'VOLUNTEER':
        return 'role-volunteer';
      default:
        return 'role-citizen';
    }
  }

  getStatusClass(status?: string): string {
    switch ((status || '').toUpperCase()) {
      case 'ACTIVE':
        return 'status-active';
      case 'PENDING':
        return 'status-pending';
      case 'SUSPENDED':
        return 'status-suspended';
      default:
        return 'status-default';
    }
  }
}
