=== FILE: \src\app\shared\components\map-picker\map-picker.component.ts ===
// src/app/shared/components/map-picker/map-picker.component.ts
import {
  Component, Input, Output, EventEmitter,
  AfterViewInit, OnDestroy, ElementRef, ViewChild
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import * as L from 'leaflet';

/**
 * Interactive map picker.
 * The user clicks anywhere on the map → emits a WKT POINT string.
 *
 * Usage:
 *   <app-map-picker
 *     [initialWkt]="sensorForm.get('location')?.value"
 *     (locationPicked)="onLocationPicked($event)">
 *   </app-map-picker>
 */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-map-picker',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule],
  template: `
    <div class="space-y-2">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-on-surface-variant font-medium">
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-sm text-primary">touch_app</span>
        Click on the map to pin the location
      </div>
      <div #mapEl style="height:320px; width:100%; border-radius:12px; overflow:hidden;"
           class="border border-white/10"></div>
      <div *ngIf="pickedWkt" class="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 bg-primary/10 border border-primary/20 rounded-lg text-xs">
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-sm">location_on</span>
        <span class="font-mono text-primary">{{ pickedWkt }}</span>
        <button type="button" (click)="clearPin()"
                class="ml-auto text-error hover:text-error/70 transition-colors">
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-sm">close</span>
        </button>
      </div>
      <div *ngIf="!pickedWkt" class="text-xs text-on-surface-variant/60 italic">
        No location selected — click the map above
      </div>
    </div>
  `
})
export class MapPickerComponent implements AfterViewInit, OnDestroy {
  @ViewChild('mapEl', { static: true }) mapEl!: ElementRef;

  /** Pre-populate from an existing WKT point, e.g. "POINT(10.5 36.8)" */
  @Input() set initialWkt(wkt: string | null | undefined) {
    if (wkt) {
      this._initialWkt = wkt;
      this.pickedWkt   = wkt;
      this._tryShowPin(wkt);
    }
  }

  /** Emits a WKT string every time the user picks a point */
  @Output() locationPicked = new EventEmitter<string>();

  pickedWkt: string | null = null;
  private _initialWkt: string | null = null;
  private map!: L.Map;
  private marker?: L.Marker;

  // Default center: Tunis
  private center: [number, number] = [36.8065, 10.1815];

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.map = L.map(this.mapEl.nativeElement, {
      zoomControl: false,
      scrollWheelZoom: true,
      doubleClickZoom: false,
    }).setView(this.center, 9);

    L.control.zoom({ position: 'topright' }).addTo(this.map);

    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/dark_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png', {
      attribution: '© OSM © CartoDB',
      subdomains: 'abcd',
      maxZoom: 19,
    }).addTo(this.map);

    // Click → place / move pin
    this.map.on('click', (e: L.LeafletMouseEvent) => {
      const { lat, lng } = e.latlng;
      this._placePin(lat, lng);
      const wkt = `POINT(${lng.toFixed(6)} ${lat.toFixed(6)})`;
      this.pickedWkt = wkt;
      this.locationPicked.emit(wkt);
    });

    // If initial WKT was provided before map was ready, show pin now
    if (this._initialWkt) this._tryShowPin(this._initialWkt);

    setTimeout(() => this.map?.invalidateSize(), 100);
  }

  clearPin(): void {
    if (this.marker) { this.map.removeLayer(this.marker); this.marker = undefined; }
    this.pickedWkt = null;
    this.locationPicked.emit('');
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void { this.map?.remove(); }

  private _placePin(lat: number, lng: number): void {
    if (this.marker) this.map.removeLayer(this.marker);
    this.marker = L.marker([lat, lng], {
      icon: L.divIcon({
        className: '',
        html: `<div style="
          width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:50% 50% 50% 0;
          background:#6C5CE7;border:3px solid #fff;
          transform:rotate(-45deg);
          box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(108,92,231,0.6)">
        </div>`,
        iconSize:   [28, 28],
        iconAnchor: [14, 28],
      })
    }).addTo(this.map);
  }

  private _tryShowPin(wkt: string): void {
    if (!this.map) return;
    const m = wkt.match(/POINT\(([^ ]+) ([^ )]+)\)/i);
    if (m) {
      const lng = parseFloat(m[1]);
      const lat = parseFloat(m[2]);
      this._placePin(lat, lng);
      this.map.setView([lat, lng], 13);
    }
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\shared\components\map-view\map-view.component.ts ===
// src/app/shared/components/map-view/map-view.component.ts
import { Component, Input, AfterViewInit, OnChanges, OnDestroy, SimpleChanges, ElementRef, ViewChild } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import * as L from 'leaflet';
import wkt from "wkt";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-map-view',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule],
  template: `<div #mapContainer style="height: 100%; width: 100%;"></div>`,
  styles: [`:host { display: block; height: 100%; width: 100%; }`]
})
export class MapViewComponent implements AfterViewInit, OnChanges, OnDestroy {
  @ViewChild('mapContainer', { static: true }) mapContainer!: ElementRef;
  @Input() center: [number, number] = [36.8065, 10.1815];
  @Input() zoom: number = 8;
  @Input() geometries: Array<{ type: string; wkt: string; color?: string }> = [];

  private map!: L.Map;
  private layers: L.Layer[] = [];
  private lastGeometriesHash: string = '';

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.initMap();
    this.updateGeometries();
    setTimeout(() => this.map?.invalidateSize(), 100);
  }

  ngOnChanges(changes: SimpleChanges): void {
    if (changes['geometries'] && this.map) {
      const newHash = JSON.stringify(this.geometries);
      if (newHash !== this.lastGeometriesHash) {
        this.lastGeometriesHash = newHash;
        this.updateGeometries();
      }
    }
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.map) this.map.remove();
  }

  private initMap(): void {
    this.map = L.map(this.mapContainer.nativeElement, {
      zoomControl: true,
      scrollWheelZoom: true,
      doubleClickZoom: true,
      dragging: true,
      touchZoom: true,
      boxZoom: true,
      keyboard: true
    }).setView(this.center, this.zoom);

    L.control.zoom({ position: 'topright' }).addTo(this.map);

    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png', {
      attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OSM</a> &copy; CartoDB',
      subdomains: 'abcd',
      maxZoom: 19,
      minZoom: 1
    }).addTo(this.map);
  }

  private updateGeometries(): void {
    if (!this.map) return;
    this.clearLayers();
    this.addGeometries();
  }

  private clearLayers(): void {
    this.layers.forEach(layer => this.map?.removeLayer(layer));
    this.layers = [];
  }

  private addGeometries(): void {
    if (!this.map) return;

    this.geometries.forEach(geo => {
      try {
        const geom = wkt.parse(geo.wkt);
        let layer: L.Layer | null = null;
        if (geom.type === 'Point') {
          layer = L.marker([geom.coordinates[1], geom.coordinates[0]]);
        } else if (geom.type === 'LineString') {
          const latLngs = geom.coordinates.map((coord: number[]) => [coord[1], coord[0]]);
          layer = L.polyline(latLngs, { color: geo.color || '#3388ff', weight: 4 });
        } else if (geom.type === 'Polygon') {
          const latLngs = geom.coordinates[0].map((coord: number[]) => [coord[1], coord[0]]);
          layer = L.polygon(latLngs, { color: geo.color || '#ff3388', fillOpacity: 0.3 });
        }
        if (layer) {
          layer.addTo(this.map);
          this.layers.push(layer);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Invalid WKT:', geo.wkt, e);
      }
    });

    // Fit bounds only on initial load, not on every update
    if (this.layers.length && this.map && !this.lastGeometriesHash) {
      const group = L.featureGroup(this.layers);
      const bounds = group.getBounds();
      if (bounds.isValid()) {
        this.map.fitBounds(bounds);
      }
    }
  }
}

import { Component, EventEmitter, Input, OnInit, OnChanges, Output } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { Router, RouterModule, RouterLinkActive } from '@angular/router';
import { NavItem } from '../../models/nav-item.model';
import { AuthService } from '../../../core/services/auth.service';

const ADMIN_ROLES = ['PLATFORM_ADMIN', 'ORG_ADMIN', 'AGENCY_ADMIN', 'ADMIN'];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-sidebar',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule, RouterLinkActive],
  templateUrl: './sidebar.component.html',

export class SidebarComponent implements OnInit, OnChanges {
  @Input() role: string = 'PLATFORM_ADMIN';

  userName: string = 'User';

  navItems: NavItem[] = [];

    { label: 'Alerts',        icon: 'emergency',      route: '/alerts'              },
    { label: 'Communication', icon: 'forum',          route: '/communication/chat'  },
    { label: 'My Profile',    icon: 'person',         route: '/frontoffice-profile' },
  ];

  get isAdmin(): boolean {
    return ADMIN_ROLES.includes((this.role || '').trim().toUpperCase());
  }

  get accentColor(): string {
    return this.isAdmin ? '#3B82F6' : '#aca3ff';
  }

  get ctaRoute(): string {
    return this.isAdmin ? '/admin/incidents/new' : '/report';
  }

  get ctaLabel(): string {
    return this.isAdmin ? 'New Incident' : 'Report Incident';
  }

  constructor(private authService: AuthService, private router: Router) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const roleFromService = (this.authService.getRole() || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!this.role || this.role === 'PLATFORM_ADMIN') {
      this.role = roleFromService;
    }
    this.userRole = this.role;
    this.userName = localStorage.getItem('email')?.split('@')[0] || 'User';
    this.updateNavItems();
  }

  ngOnChanges(): void {
    this.updateNavItems();
  }

  private updateNavItems(): void {
    this.navItems = this.isAdmin ? this.adminNav : this.userNav;
  }

  onLogout(): void {
    localStorage.clear();
    this.authService.logout();
    this.router.navigateByUrl('/landing');
  }
}

=== FILE: \src\app\core\guards\auth.guard.ts ===
import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CanActivateFn, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../services/auth.service';

const ADMIN_ROLES = ['PLATFORM_ADMIN', 'ORG_ADMIN', 'AGENCY_ADMIN', 'ADMIN'];

export const authGuard: CanActivateFn = (route, state) => {
  const authService = inject(AuthService);
  const router = inject(Router);

  const token = authService.getToken();
  if (!token) {
    router.navigateByUrl('/login');
    return false;
  }

  return true;
};

/** Guard that allows only admin roles */
export const adminGuard: CanActivateFn = (route, state) => {
  const authService = inject(AuthService);
  const router = inject(Router);

  const token = authService.getToken();
  if (!token) {
    router.navigateByUrl('/login');
    return false;
  }

  const role = (authService.getRole() || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!ADMIN_ROLES.includes(role)) {
    router.navigateByUrl('/home');
    return false;
  }

  return true;
};


=== FILE: \src\app\core\guards\role.guard.ts ===
import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CanActivateFn, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../services/auth.service';

export const roleGuard: CanActivateFn = (route, state) => {
  const authService = inject(AuthService);
  const router = inject(Router);

  const requiredRoles = route.data?.['roles'] as Array<string>;
  const userRole = (authService.getRole() || '').trim().toUpperCase();

  if (requiredRoles && requiredRoles.includes(userRole)) {
    return true;
  }

  // Non-admin users go to the citizen home, not a broken /unauthorized route
  router.navigate(['/home']);
  return false;
};

=== FILE: \src\app\core\interceptors\auth.interceptor.ts ===
import { HttpErrorResponse, HttpInterceptorFn } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { catchError, throwError } from 'rxjs';

export const authInterceptor: HttpInterceptorFn = (req, next) => {
  const router = inject(Router);
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');

  const isAuthRequest =
    req.url.includes('/auth/login') || req.url.includes('/auth/register');

  const authReq = token && !isAuthRequest
    ? req.clone({
        setHeaders: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
        }
      })
    : req;

  return next(authReq).pipe(
    catchError((error: HttpErrorResponse) => {
      if (error.status === 401) {
        localStorage.clear();
        router.navigate(['/login']);
      }
      return throwError(() => error);
    })
  );
};

  }

    return this.http.post<Alert>('/alerts', alert);
  }

  updateAlert(id: number, alert: Partial<Alert>): Observable<Alert> {
    return this.http.put<Alert>(`/alerts/${id}`, alert);
  }

  deleteAlert(id: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`/alerts/${id}`);
  }

  resolveAlert(id: number): Observable<Alert> {
    return this.http.patch<Alert>(`/alerts/${id}/resolve`, {});
  }

  // Geofence Zones
  getAllZones(): Observable<GeofenceZone[]> {
    return this.http.get<GeofenceZone[]>('/geofence-zones');
  }

  getZoneById(id: number): Observable<GeofenceZone> {
    return this.http.get<GeofenceZone>(`/geofence-zones/${id}`);
  }

  getZonesByAlert(alertId: number): Observable<GeofenceZone[]> {
    return this.http.get<GeofenceZone[]>(`/geofence-zones/alert/${alertId}`);
  }

    return this.http.post<GeofenceZone>('/geofence-zones', zone);
  }

  updateZone(id: number, zone: Partial<GeofenceZone>): Observable<GeofenceZone> {
    return this.http.put<GeofenceZone>(`/geofence-zones/${id}`, zone);
  }

  deleteZone(id: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`/geofence-zones/${id}`);
  }
}

=== FILE: \src\app\core\services\auth.service.ts ===
// src/app/core/services/auth.service.ts
import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { Observable, tap, catchError, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { HttpService } from './http.service';

export interface AuthResponse {
  token: string;
  tokenType?: string;
  userId?: number;
  id?: number;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  message?: string;
}

export interface LogoutResponse {
  message: string;
}

export interface LoginRequest {
  email: string;
  password: string;
}

export interface RegisterRequest {
  email: string;
  password: string;
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  role?: string;
  phoneNumber?: string;
}

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class AuthService {
  constructor(private httpService: HttpService) {
    console.log('AuthService initialized with HttpService');
  }

  register(data: RegisterRequest): Observable<AuthResponse> {
    const registrationData = {
      ...data,
      role: data.role || 'VOLUNTEER'  // Changed from 'CITIZEN' to match your app
    };

    console.log('📝 Registering user:', registrationData.email);

    // Try different endpoint paths based on your backend configuration
    const endpoints = [
      '/auth/register',      // Direct
      '/api/auth/register',  // With /api prefix
      '/api/v1/auth/register' // With /api/v1 prefix
    ];

    // Use the first endpoint, we'll test others if this fails
    const endpoint = '/auth/register';
    console.log('📍 Register endpoint:', endpoint);

    return this.httpService.post<AuthResponse>(endpoint, registrationData).pipe(
      tap(res => {
        console.log('✅ Registration API response:', res);
        this.storeAuthData(res);
      }),
      catchError(error => {
        console.error('❌ Registration API error:', error);
        console.log('💡 Tip: Check if your backend endpoint is correct. The server returned HTML instead of JSON.');
        return throwError(() => error);
      })
    );
  }

  login(data: LoginRequest): Observable<AuthResponse> {
    console.log('🔐 Logging in user:', data.email);
    const endpoint = '/auth/login';
    console.log('📍 Login endpoint:', endpoint);

    return this.httpService.post<AuthResponse>(endpoint, data).pipe(
      tap(res => {
        console.log('✅ Login API response:', res);
        this.storeAuthData(res);
      }),
      catchError(error => {
        console.error('❌ Login API error:', error);
        return throwError(() => error);
      })
    );
  }

  logout(): Observable<LogoutResponse> {
    console.log('🚪 Logging out');
    const endpoint = '/auth/logout';
    return this.httpService.post<LogoutResponse>(endpoint, {}).pipe(
      tap(() => {
        console.log('✅ Logout successful');
        this.clearAuthData();
      }),
      catchError((error) => {
        console.error('❌ Logout error:', error);
        this.clearAuthData();
        return throwError(() => error);
      })
    );
  }

  private storeAuthData(res: AuthResponse): void {
    if (res.token) {
      localStorage.setItem('token', res.token);
    }

    const userId = res.userId || res.id;
    if (userId) {
      localStorage.setItem('userId', userId.toString());
    }

    if (res.email) {
      localStorage.setItem('email', res.email);
    }

    if (res.role) {
      localStorage.setItem('role', res.role);
    }

    console.log('💾 Auth data stored - Role:', res.role, 'UserId:', userId);
  }

  clearAuthData(): void {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('userId');
    localStorage.removeItem('email');
    localStorage.removeItem('role');
    console.log('🗑️ Auth data cleared');
  }

  getToken(): string | null {
    return localStorage.getItem('token');
  }

  getUserId(): string | null {
    return localStorage.getItem('userId');
  }

  getEmail(): string | null {
    return localStorage.getItem('email');
  }

  getRole(): string | null {
    return localStorage.getItem('role');
  }

  isLoggedIn(): boolean {
    const token = this.getToken();
    const isLoggedIn = !!token;
    console.log('🔍 Is logged in:', isLoggedIn);
    return isLoggedIn;
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\core\services\chat.service.ts ===
import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpHeaders } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Client, IMessage, StompSubscription } from '@stomp/stompjs';
import SockJS from 'sockjs-client';
import { Observable, Subject } from 'rxjs';
import { environment } from '../../../environments/environment';
import { Message, MessageRequest } from '../../modules/communication/models/message.model';
import { Conversation } from '../../modules/communication/models/conversation.model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class ChatService {
  private apiUrl = environment.apiUrl;
  private stompClient: Client | null = null;
  private messageSubject = new Subject<Message>();

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {}

  private getAuthHeaders(): HttpHeaders {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    return new HttpHeaders({
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
    });
  }

  getMyConversations(): Observable<Conversation[]> {
    return this.http.get<Conversation[]>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/communication/conversations/me`,
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }

  getMessagesByConversation(conversationId: number): Observable<Message[]> {
    return this.http.get<Message[]>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/communication/messages/conversation/${conversationId}`,
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }

  sendMessage(payload: MessageRequest): Observable<Message> {
    return this.http.post<Message>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/communication/messages/add`,
      payload,
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }

  connectToConversation(conversationId: number): void {
    if (this.stompClient?.active) {
      this.stompClient.deactivate();
    }

    this.stompClient = new Client({
      webSocketFactory: () => new SockJS(`${this.apiUrl}/ws-chat`),
      reconnectDelay: 5000,
      debug: (str) => console.log('STOMP:', str)
    });

    this.stompClient.onConnect = () => {
      this.stompClient?.subscribe(
        `/topic/conversations/${conversationId}`,
        (message: IMessage) => {
          const chatMessage: Message = JSON.parse(message.body);
          this.messageSubject.next(chatMessage);
        }
      );
    };

    this.stompClient.onStompError = (frame) => {
      console.error('Broker error:', frame);
    };

    this.stompClient.activate();
  }

  sendTyping(conversationId: number): void {
    if (!this.stompClient || !this.stompClient.active) {
      return;
    }

    this.stompClient.publish({
      destination: '/app/typing',
      body: JSON.stringify({
        conversationId,
        userId: Number(localStorage.getItem('userId')),
        email: localStorage.getItem('email')
      })
    });
  }

  onTyping(conversationId: number): Observable<any> {
    return new Observable<any>((observer) => {
      let subscription: StompSubscription | null = null;

      const waitForConnection = setInterval(() => {
        if (this.stompClient && this.stompClient.active) {
          clearInterval(waitForConnection);

          subscription = this.stompClient.subscribe(
            `/topic/typing/${conversationId}`,
            (msg: IMessage) => {
              observer.next(JSON.parse(msg.body));
            }
          );
        }
      }, 200);

      return () => {
        clearInterval(waitForConnection);
        if (subscription) {
          subscription.unsubscribe();
        }
      };
    });
  }

  onMessageReceived(): Observable<Message> {
    return this.messageSubject.asObservable();
  }

  disconnect(): void {
    if (this.stompClient?.active) {
      this.stompClient.deactivate();
    }
  }
}

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class CoordinationService {
  private helpBase = `${environment.apiUrl}/help-requests`;
  private taskBase = `${environment.apiUrl}/assignments`;
  private offerBase = `${environment.apiUrl}/volunteer-offers`;

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {}



  completeAssignment(id: number, note: string, rating: number): Observable<TaskAssignment> {

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpHeaders } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { environment } from '../../../environments/environment';
import {
  AiPrediction,
  DiscussionRequest,
  DiscussionRequestCreate
} from '../../modules/communication/models/discussion-request.model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class DiscussionRequestService {
  private apiUrl = `${environment.apiUrl}/api/conversation-requests`;

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {}

  private getAuthHeaders(): HttpHeaders {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    return new HttpHeaders({
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
    });
  }

  createRequest(citizenId: number, payload: DiscussionRequestCreate): Observable<DiscussionRequest> {
    return this.http.post<DiscussionRequest>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/citizen/${citizenId}`,
      payload,
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }

  getPendingRequests(): Observable<DiscussionRequest[]> {
    return this.http.get<DiscussionRequest[]>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/pending`,
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }

  getRequestsByCitizen(citizenId: number): Observable<DiscussionRequest[]> {
    return this.http.get<DiscussionRequest[]>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/citizen/${citizenId}`,
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }

  getRequestsByAdmin(adminId: number): Observable<DiscussionRequest[]> {
    return this.http.get<DiscussionRequest[]>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/admin/${adminId}`,
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }

  acceptRequest(requestId: number, adminId: number): Observable<DiscussionRequest> {
    return this.http.put<DiscussionRequest>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/${requestId}/accept/admin/${adminId}`,
      {},
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }

  rejectRequest(requestId: number, adminId: number): Observable<DiscussionRequest> {
    return this.http.put<DiscussionRequest>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/${requestId}/reject/admin/${adminId}`,
      {},
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }

  predictRequest(data: { subject: string; description: string }): Observable<AiPrediction> {
    return this.http.post<AiPrediction>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/predict`,
      data,
      { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() }
    );
  }
}

=== FILE: \src\app\core\services\http.service.ts ===
import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpHeaders, HttpParams, HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { catchError } from 'rxjs/operators';
import { environment } from '../../../environments/environment';
import {T} from '@angular/cdk/keycodes';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class HttpService {
  private readonly apiUrl = environment.apiUrl;

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {
    console.log('🌐 HttpService initialized with API URL:', this.apiUrl);
  }

  private buildHeaders(additional?: HttpHeaders, isFormData = false): HttpHeaders {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    let headers = new HttpHeaders();

    // Only set JSON content-type when NOT sending FormData
    if (!isFormData) {
      headers = headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
      headers = headers.set('Accept', 'application/json');
    }
    if (token) headers = headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
    if (additional) {
      additional.keys().forEach(key => {
        const val = additional.get(key);
        if (val !== null) {
          headers = headers.set(key, val);
        }
      });
    }

    return headers;
  }

  private handleError(error: HttpErrorResponse) {
    // Check if the response is HTML instead of JSON
    let errorMessage = error.message;
    let isHtmlResponse = false;

    if (error.error && typeof error.error === 'string' && error.error.trim().startsWith('<!DOCTYPE')) {
      isHtmlResponse = true;
      errorMessage = `Server returned HTML instead of JSON. The endpoint ${error.url} may not exist.`;
    } else if (error.error?.message) {
      errorMessage = error.error.message;
    }

    console.error('HTTP Error:', {
      url: error.url,
      status: error.status,
      message: errorMessage,
      isHtmlResponse: isHtmlResponse
    });

    if (isHtmlResponse) {
      console.error('💡 The backend endpoint might be incorrect. Check your Spring Boot controller mappings.');
    }

    return throwError(() => error);
  }

  get<T>(endpoint: string, params?: { polygon: string }, customHeaders?: HttpHeaders): Observable<T> {
    const options = {
      headers: this.buildHeaders(customHeaders, false),
      params
    };
    return this.http.get<T>(`${this.apiUrl}${endpoint}`, options);}


  post<T>(endpoint: string, body: unknown, params?: HttpParams, customHeaders?: HttpHeaders): Observable<T> {
    const url = `${this.apiUrl}${endpoint}`;
    const isFormData = body instanceof FormData;
    console.log('📡 POST Request:', url, body);
    return this.http.post<T>(url, body as any, { headers: this.buildHeaders(customHeaders, isFormData), params }).pipe(catchError(this.handleError));
  }

  put<T>(endpoint: string, body: unknown, params?: HttpParams, customHeaders?: HttpHeaders): Observable<T> {
    const url = `${this.apiUrl}${endpoint}`;
    const isFormData = body instanceof FormData;
    return this.http.put<T>(url, body as any, { headers: this.buildHeaders(customHeaders, isFormData), params }).pipe(catchError(this.handleError));
  }


  patch<T>(endpoint: string, body: unknown, params?: HttpParams, customHeaders?: HttpHeaders): Observable<T> {
    const isFormData = body instanceof FormData;
    const options = {
      headers: this.buildHeaders(customHeaders, isFormData),
      params
    };
    return this.http.patch<T>(`${this.apiUrl}${endpoint}`, body as any, options);
  }
  delete<T>(endpoint: string, params?: HttpParams, customHeaders?: HttpHeaders): Observable<T> {
    const url = `${this.apiUrl}${endpoint}`;
    return this.http.delete<T>(url, { headers: this.buildHeaders(customHeaders), params }).pipe(catchError(this.handleError));
  }
}

import { map } from 'rxjs/operators';
import { HttpService } from './http.service'; // ← use master's HttpService, not HttpClient directly

export interface IncidentReport {
  id?: number;
  reporterId?: number;
  alertId?: number;
  disasterType: string;
  severity: string;
  location: string;
  description: string;
  status?: string;
  credibilityScore?: number;
  upvoteCount?: number;
  createdAt?: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

export interface ReportEvidence {
  id?: number;
  incidentReportId?: number;
  fileUrl: string;
  fileType?: string;
  clipScore?: number;        // ✅ Added missing field
  capturedAt?: string;
}

  id: number;
  fromStatus: string | null;
  toStatus: string;
  changedBy: number | null;
  note: string | null;
  changedAt: string;
}

interface ApiResponse<T> {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  data: T;
}

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class IncidentService {

  // All paths are relative — HttpService prepends environment.apiUrl (port 8089)
  private base         = '/incidents';
  private evidenceBase = '/evidences';

  constructor(private http: HttpService) {}

  getAll(): Observable<IncidentReport[]> {
    return this.http.get<ApiResponse<IncidentReport[]>>(this.base)
      .pipe(map(r => r.data));
  }

  getById(id: number): Observable<IncidentReport> {
    return this.http.get<ApiResponse<IncidentReport>>(`${this.base}/${id}`)
      .pipe(map(r => r.data));
  }

  // ✅ NEW: GET incidents by status
  getByStatus(status: string): Observable<IncidentReport[]> {
    return this.http.get<ApiResponse<IncidentReport[]>>(`${this.base}/status/${status}`)
      .pipe(map(r => r.data));
  }

  // ✅ NEW: GET incidents by severity
  getBySeverity(severity: string): Observable<IncidentReport[]> {
    return this.http.get<ApiResponse<IncidentReport[]>>(`${this.base}/severity/${severity}`)
      .pipe(map(r => r.data));
  }

  // ✅ Create new incident (accepts partial for flexibility)
  create(report: Partial<IncidentReport>): Observable<IncidentReport> {
    return this.http.post<ApiResponse<IncidentReport>>(this.base, report)
      .pipe(map(r => r.data));
  }

  // ✅ Update incident (accepts partial for flexibility)
  update(id: number, report: Partial<IncidentReport>): Observable<IncidentReport> {
    return this.http.put<ApiResponse<IncidentReport>>(`${this.base}/${id}`, report)
      .pipe(map(r => r.data));
  }

  // ✅ Delete incident
  delete(id: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<ApiResponse<void>>(`${this.base}/${id}`)
      .pipe(map(() => void 0));
  }

  // ✅ Upvote incident
  upvote(id: number): Observable<IncidentReport> {
    return this.http.post<ApiResponse<IncidentReport>>(`${this.base}/${id}/upvote`, {})
      .pipe(map(r => r.data));
  }

  // ✅ Downvote incident
  downvote(id: number): Observable<IncidentReport> {
    return this.http.post<ApiResponse<IncidentReport>>(`${this.base}/${id}/downvote`, {})
      .pipe(map(r => r.data));
  }

  // ✅ Update incident status
  updateStatus(id: number, status: string): Observable<IncidentReport> {
    return this.http.post<ApiResponse<IncidentReport>>(
      `${this.base}/${id}/status?status=${status}`, {}
    ).pipe(map(r => r.data));
  }

  checkDuplicate(dto: Partial<IncidentReport>): Observable<{ message: string; data: IncidentReport | null }> {
    return this.http.post<ApiResponse<IncidentReport | null>>(
      `${this.base}/check-duplicate`, dto
    ).pipe(map(r => ({ message: r.message, data: r.data })));
  }

  // ✅ NEW: Find duplicate (convenience method)
  findDuplicate(dto: Partial<IncidentReport>): Observable<IncidentReport | null> {
    return this.checkDuplicate(dto).pipe(map(r => r.data));

  }

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { HttpService } from './http.service';
import { environment } from '../../../environments/environment';

export interface SpatialSensor {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  type: string;
  location: string;
  status: string;
  manufacturer: string | null;
}

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class SpatialService {
  private readonly apiUrl = environment.apiUrl;

  constructor(
    private http: HttpService,      // keep for other endpoints if needed
    private httpClient: HttpClient  // add this
  ) {}

  getSensorsInsideZone(polygonWkt: string): Observable<SpatialSensor[]> {
    const params = new HttpParams().set('polygon', polygonWkt);
    // Use httpClient directly to bypass restrictive HttpService
    return this.httpClient.get<SpatialSensor[]>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/spatial/sensors/inside-zone`,
      { params }
    );
  }

  getNearestSensors(pointWkt: string, limit: number = 5): Observable<SpatialSensor[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams()
      .set('point', pointWkt)
      .set('limit', limit.toString());
    // Use httpClient directly
    return this.httpClient.get<SpatialSensor[]>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/spatial/sensors/nearest`,
      { params }
    );
  }
}

=== FILE: \src\app\layouts\authenticated-layout\authenticated-layout.component.ts ===
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { Router, RouterOutlet } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/services/auth.service';
import { SidebarComponent } from '../../shared/components/sidebar/sidebar.component';
import { HeaderComponent } from '../../shared/components/header/header.component';
import { MobileNavComponent } from '../../shared/components/mobile-nav/mobile-nav.component';

const ADMIN_ROLES = ['PLATFORM_ADMIN', 'ORG_ADMIN', 'AGENCY_ADMIN', 'ADMIN'];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-authenticated-layout',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterOutlet, HeaderComponent, MobileNavComponent, SidebarComponent],
  templateUrl: './authenticated-layout.component.html',
})
export class AuthenticatedLayoutComponent implements OnInit {
  currentRole: string = 'PLATFORM_ADMIN';

  constructor(private authService: AuthService, private router: Router) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const role = (this.authService.getRole() || '').trim().toUpperCase();

    if (!ADMIN_ROLES.includes(role)) {
      this.router.navigateByUrl('/home');
      return;
    }

    this.currentRole = ADMIN_ROLES.includes(role) ? role : 'ADMIN';
  }

  logout(): void {
    this.authService.logout().subscribe({
      next: () => this.router.navigate(['/login']),
      error: () => this.router.navigate(['/login']),
    });
  }
}

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpHeaders } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { environment } from '../../../../environments/environment';
import {
  CheckInPayload,
  ResourceEntity,
  ResourcePayload,
  ShelterEntity,
  ShelterOccupantEntity,
  ShelterPayload,
  UserEntity,
} from '../../../shared/models/resource-shelter/resource-shelter.model';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class ResourceShelterService {
  private readonly apiUrl = environment.apiUrl;

  private readonly baseUrl = environment.apiUrl.replace('/api', '');

  constructor(private readonly http: HttpClient) {}

  private get headers(): { headers: HttpHeaders } {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    let h = new HttpHeaders({
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Accept': 'application/json'
    });
    if (token) {
      h = h.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
    }
    return { headers: h };
  }

  getAllResources(): Observable<ResourceEntity[]> {
    return this.http.get<ResourceEntity[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/resources`, this.headers);
  }

  createResource(payload: ResourcePayload): Observable<ResourceEntity> {
    return this.http.post<ResourceEntity>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/resources`, payload, this.headers);
  }

  updateResource(resourceId: number, payload: ResourcePayload): Observable<ResourceEntity> {
    return this.http.put<ResourceEntity>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/resources/${resourceId}`, payload, this.headers);
  }

  deleteResource(resourceId: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/resources/${resourceId}`, this.headers);
  }

  getAllShelters(): Observable<ShelterEntity[]> {
    return this.http.get<ShelterEntity[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/shelters`, this.headers);
  }

  createShelter(payload: ShelterPayload): Observable<ShelterEntity> {
    return this.http.post<ShelterEntity>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/shelters`, payload, this.headers);
  }

  updateShelter(shelterId: number, payload: ShelterPayload): Observable<ShelterEntity> {
    return this.http.put<ShelterEntity>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/shelters/${shelterId}`, payload, this.headers);
  }

  deleteShelter(shelterId: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/shelters/${shelterId}`, this.headers);
  }

  getAllUsers(): Observable<UserEntity[]> {
    return this.http.get<UserEntity[]>(`${this.apiUrl}/identity-access/users`, this.headers);
  }

  createUser(name: string): Observable<UserEntity> {
    return this.http.post<UserEntity>(`${this.apiUrl}/identity-access/users`, { 
      firstName: name, 
      lastName: 'Agency', 
      email: `agency_${Date.now()}@test.com`, 
      password: 'Password123!', 
      role: 'AGENCY_ADMIN' 
    }, this.headers);
  }

  checkInShelter(payload: CheckInPayload): Observable<ShelterOccupantEntity> {
    return this.http.post<ShelterOccupantEntity>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/shelter-occupants/check-in`, payload, this.headers);
  }

  getShelterOccupantsByShelter(shelterId: number): Observable<ShelterOccupantEntity[]> {
    return this.http.get<ShelterOccupantEntity[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/shelter-occupants?shelterId=${shelterId}`, this.headers);
  }

  checkOutShelter(recordId: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.post<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/resource-shelter/shelter-occupants/${recordId}/check-out`, {}, this.headers);
  }
}


=== FILE: \src\app\core\services\reviews-trust\reviews-trust.service.ts ===
import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpHeaders } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { map } from 'rxjs/operators';
import { environment } from '../../../../environments/environment';
import {
  CreateModerationActionPayload,
  CreateReviewPayload,
  EntityType,
  ModerationActionEntity,
  ReviewEntity,
  ReviewStatus,
  ReviewTarget,
  TrustScoreEntity,
} from '../../../shared/models/reviews/reviews-trust.model';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class ReviewsTrustService {
  private readonly apiUrl = environment.apiUrl;
  private readonly baseUrl = environment.apiUrl.replace('/api', '');

  constructor(private readonly http: HttpClient) {}

  private get headers(): { headers: HttpHeaders } {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    let h = new HttpHeaders({
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Accept': 'application/json'
    });
    if (token) {
      h = h.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
    }
    return { headers: h };
  }

  getAllReviews(): Observable<ReviewEntity[]> {
    return this.http
      .get<ReviewEntity[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/reviews`, this.headers)
      .pipe(map((items) => items.map((item) => this.fromApiReview(item))));
  }

  getReviewsByTarget(targetId: number, targetType: ReviewTarget): Observable<ReviewEntity[]> {
    const apiTargetType = this.toApiTargetType(targetType);
    return this.http.get<ReviewEntity[]>(
      `${this.baseUrl}/reviews?targetId=${targetId}&targetType=${apiTargetType}`,
      this.headers
    ).pipe(map((items) => items.map((item) => this.fromApiReview(item))));
  }

  createReview(payload: CreateReviewPayload): Observable<ReviewEntity> {
    const body: CreateReviewPayload = {
      ...payload,
      targetType: this.toApiTargetType(payload.targetType),
    };
    return this.http
      .post<ReviewEntity>(`${this.baseUrl}/reviews`, body, this.headers)
      .pipe(map((item) => this.fromApiReview(item)));
  }

  moderateReview(reviewId: number, status: ReviewStatus): Observable<ReviewEntity> {
    return this.http
      .patch<ReviewEntity>(`${this.baseUrl}/reviews/${reviewId}/moderate?status=${status}`, null, this.headers)
      .pipe(map((item) => this.fromApiReview(item)));
  }

  createModerationAction(payload: CreateModerationActionPayload): Observable<ModerationActionEntity> {
    return this.http.post<ModerationActionEntity>(
      `${this.baseUrl}/reviews/moderation`,
      payload,
      this.headers,
    );
  }

  getTrustScoreByEntity(entityId: number, entityType: EntityType): Observable<TrustScoreEntity> {
    const apiEntityType = this.toApiTargetType(entityType);
    return this.http.get<TrustScoreEntity>(
      `${this.baseUrl}/reviews/trust-scores/by-entity?entityId=${entityId}&entityType=${apiEntityType}`,
      this.headers
    );
  }

  recalculateTrustScore(entityId: number, entityType: EntityType): Observable<TrustScoreEntity> {
    const apiEntityType = this.toApiTargetType(entityType);
    return this.http.post<TrustScoreEntity>(
      `${this.baseUrl}/reviews/trust-scores/recalculate?entityId=${entityId}&entityType=${apiEntityType}`,
      null,
      this.headers
    );
  }

  private toApiTargetType(targetType: ReviewTarget): ReviewTarget {
    if (targetType === 'SHELTER') {
      return 'VOLUNTEER';
    }
    return targetType;
  }

  private fromApiReview(item: ReviewEntity): ReviewEntity {
    if (item.targetType === 'VOLUNTEER') {
      return { ...item, targetType: 'SHELTER' };
    }
    return item;
  }
}

=== FILE: \src\app\modules\identity\social-auth-success\social-auth-success.component.ts ===
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-social-auth-success',
  standalone: true,
  template: `<p>Connexion en cours...</p>`
})
export class SocialAuthSuccessComponent implements OnInit {
  constructor(private router: Router) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);

    const token = params.get('token');
    const userId = params.get('userId');
    const email = params.get('email');
    const role = params.get('role');
    const error = params.get('error');

    if (error) {
      this.router.navigateByUrl('/login');
      return;
    }

    if (token && userId && email && role) {
      localStorage.setItem('token', token);
      localStorage.setItem('userId', userId);
      localStorage.setItem('email', email);
      localStorage.setItem('role', role);

      const normalizedRole = role.trim().toUpperCase();
      const adminRoles = ['PLATFORM_ADMIN', 'ORG_ADMIN', 'AGENCY_ADMIN'];

      if (adminRoles.includes(normalizedRole)) {
        this.router.navigateByUrl('/dashboard');
      } else {
        this.router.navigateByUrl('/landing');
      }
    } else {
      this.router.navigateByUrl('/login');
    }
  }
}
